});

test("Git synthetic DTO boundaries reject oversized patch, extra fields, duplicate rows and mismatched root", async () => {
  const h = setup();
  h.fixture.setGitState("ready");
  const status = await gitStatusWire(h),
    detail = await gitDiffWire(h, status);
  assert.throws(() =>
    gitStatusDto({
      ...status.value,
      repository: { ...status.value.repository, worktreePath: "/wrong" },
    }),
  );
  assert.throws(() =>
    gitStatusDto({
      ...status.value,
      files: [...status.value.files, status.value.files[0]],
    }),
  );
  assert.throws(() =>
    gitStatusDto({
      ...status.value,
      files: Array(gitLimits.files + 1).fill(status.value.files[0]),
    }),
  );
  assert.throws(() =>
    gitDetailDto({
      ...detail.value,
      patch: "x".repeat(gitLimits.patchBytes + 1),
    }),
  );
  assert.throws(() =>
    gitDetailDto({
      ...detail.value,
      patch: "x\n".repeat(gitLimits.patchLines + 1),
    }),
  );
  assert.throws(() =>
    gitDetailDto({
      ...detail.value,
      patch: "@@ -1 +1 @@\n".repeat(gitLimits.patchHunks + 1),
    }),
  );
  assert.throws(() => gitDetailDto({ ...detail.value, html: true }));
  assert.equal(
    gitDetailDto({ ...detail.value, patch: "x".repeat(gitLimits.patchBytes) })
      .patch.length,
    gitLimits.patchBytes,
  );
});

test("Git actual controller mount, poll, selection, refocus and draft editing never auto-read", async () => {
  const h = await gitController();
  try {
    h.client.start();
    await extensionFlush();
    h.refocus();
    h.client.actions.refresh();
    gitSelect(h);
    h.client.actions.select("fixture-w", "fixture-s");
    h.client.actions.editConfigurationDraft(
      "instructions",
      "SYNTHETIC LOCAL DRAFT",
    );
    for (const [key, timer] of [...h.timers])
      if (timer.delay === 1200) {
        h.timers.delete(key);
        timer.callback();
      }
    await extensionFlush();
    assert.equal(h.fixture.gitReads.length, 0);
    h.seed();
    assert.equal(await h.client.actions.observeGitReview(), true);
    assert.equal(gitCurrent(h).status, "available");
    assert.equal(gitState(h).baseRef, "");
    gitSelect(h, "base");
    assert.equal(gitCanDetail(h), false);
    h.client.actions.selectGitBase("review/diverged-local-tip");
    assert.equal(gitCanDetail(h), true);
    assert.equal(h.fixture.gitReads.length, 1);
    assert.equal(await h.client.actions.readGitDiff(), true);
    assert.equal(gitDetail(h).baseOid, "2".repeat(40));
    assert.equal(h.fixture.requests.length, 0);
  } finally {
    h.client.dispose();
  }
});

for (const lane of ["status", "diff"])
  test(`Git actual controller ${lane} READ_BUSY readiness requires explicit retry`, async () => {
    const h = await gitController(
      lane === "status" ? "busy-status" : "busy-diff",
    );
    try {
      if (lane === "diff") {
        await h.client.actions.observeGitReview();
        gitSelect(h);
      }
      const run =
        lane === "status"
          ? h.client.actions.observeGitReview
          : h.client.actions.readGitDiff;
      await run();
      assert.equal(
        lane === "status" ? gitState(h).error : gitState(h).detailError,
        "READ_BUSY",
      );
      assert.equal(
        lane === "status"
          ? gitState(h).retryReady
          : gitState(h).detailRetryReady,
        false,
      );
      const count = h.fixture.gitReads.length;
      await run();
      assert.equal(h.fixture.gitReads.length, count);
      h.fixture.gitBusy.clear();
      for (const [key, timer] of [...h.timers])
        if (timer.delay === 400) {
          h.timers.delete(key);
          timer.callback();
        }
      assert.equal(h.fixture.gitReads.length, count);
      assert.equal(await run(), true);
    } finally {
      h.client.dispose();
    }
  });

for (const lane of ["status", "diff"])
  for (const boundary of [
    "cancel",
    "epoch",
    "root",
    "trust",
    "floor",
    "held",
    "refresh",
    "selection-ABA",
  ])
    test(`Git actual controller late ${lane} after ${boundary} is inert`, async () => {
      const h = await gitController();
      try {
        if (lane === "diff") {
          await h.client.actions.observeGitReview();
          gitSelect(h);
          h.fixture.holdGitDiff = true;
        } else h.fixture.holdGitStatus = true;
        const pending =
          lane === "status"
            ? h.client.actions.observeGitReview()
            : h.client.actions.readGitDiff();
        await extensionFlush();
        if (boundary === "cancel")
          (lane === "status"
            ? h.client.actions.cancelGitReview
            : h.client.actions.cancelGitDiff)();
        if (boundary === "epoch") {
          h.fixture.snapshot.runtimeEpoch += "-new";
          h.seed(true);
        }
        if (boundary === "root") {
          h.fixture.snapshot.workspaces[0].path += "-new";
          h.seed();
        }
        if (boundary === "trust") {
          h.fixture.snapshot.workspaces[0].trusted = false;
          h.fixture.snapshot.workspaces[0].trustRevision++;
          h.seed();
        }
        if (boundary === "floor") {
          h.fixture.snapshot.revision++;
          h.seed();
        }
        if (boundary === "held") {
          h.fixture.snapshot.recovery.resources = [
            {
              workspaceId: "fixture-w",
              state: "unavailable",
              reason: "unresolved_operation",
            },
          ];
          h.seed();
        }
        if (boundary === "refresh") {
          h.fixture.holdGitStatus = false;
          await h.client.actions.observeGitReview();
        }
        if (boundary === "selection-ABA") {
          if (lane === "diff") {
            h.client.actions.selectGitPath(h.scope.otherPath);
            h.client.actions.selectGitPath(h.scope.path);
          } else {
            h.client.actions.select(null, null);
            h.client.actions.select("fixture-w", "fixture-s");
          }
        }
        h.fixture.releaseGitReads();
        assert.equal(await pending, false);
        await extensionFlush();
        if (lane === "diff") assert.equal(gitDetail(h), undefined);
        else if (boundary !== "refresh") assert.equal(gitCurrent(h), undefined);
        assert.equal(h.fixture.requests.length, 0);
      } finally {
        h.client.dispose();
      }
    });

for (const name of ["held", "global-held", "untrusted"])
  test(`Git actual controller ${name} retains editable local draft and truthful inert-read admission`, async () => {
    const h = await gitController(name);
    try {
      const text = "SYNTHETIC EDIT DURING GIT HOLD";
      h.client.actions.selectWorkspaceFile(h.scope.path);
      h.client.actions.editWorkspaceFile(text);
      assert.equal(fileDraft(h).text, text);
      assert.equal(
        h.selectors.gitReviewHeld(h.client.store.getState()),
        name !== "untrusted",
      );
      assert.equal(
        await h.client.actions.observeGitReview(),
        name === "untrusted",
      );
      assert.equal(h.fixture.requests.length, 0);
    } finally {
      h.client.dispose();
    }
  });

for (const lane of ["status", "diff"])
  for (const corruption of ["extra", "root", "epoch", "floor", "oid"])
    test(`Git actual controller rejects ${lane} response with ${corruption} mismatch`, async () => {
      const producer = setup();
      producer.fixture.setGitState("ready");
      const status = await gitStatusWire(producer);
      const detail = await gitDiffWire(producer, status, {
        mode: "base",
        baseRef: "review/diverged-local-tip",
      });
      const h = await gitController("ready", {
        read(request) {
          const selectedLane =
            request.type === "git.review.read" ? "status" : "diff";
          const value = globalThis.structuredClone(
            selectedLane === "status" ? status.value : detail.value,
          );
          if (lane === selectedLane) {
            if (corruption === "extra") value.executionAuthority = true;
            if (corruption === "root")
              value.workspacePath = "/synthetic/foreign-root";
            if (corruption === "epoch") value.runtimeEpoch = "foreign-epoch";
            if (corruption === "floor") value.revision = 0;
            if (corruption === "oid") {
              if (lane === "status") value.observationId = "not-a-digest";
              else value.baseOid = "3".repeat(40);
            }
          }
          return { ok: true, value };
        },
      });
      try {
        const acceptedStatus = await h.client.actions.observeGitReview();
        if (lane === "status") {
          assert.equal(acceptedStatus, false);
          assert.equal(gitCurrent(h), undefined);
          assert.ok(["STALE", "GIT_UNVERIFIED"].includes(gitState(h).error));
        } else {
          assert.equal(acceptedStatus, true);
          gitSelect(h, "base", h.scope.path, "review/diverged-local-tip");
          assert.equal(await h.client.actions.readGitDiff(), false);
          assert.equal(gitDetail(h), undefined);
          assert.ok(
            ["STALE", "GIT_UNVERIFIED"].includes(gitState(h).detailError),
          );
        }
      } finally {
        h.client.dispose();
        producer.transport.close();
      }
    });

for (const field of ["mode", "baseRef"])
  test(`Git actual controller ${field} ABA rejects held old detail`, async () => {
    const h = await gitController("pending-diff");
    try {
      await h.client.actions.observeGitReview();
      gitSelect(h, "base", h.scope.path, "review/diverged-local-tip");
      const pending = h.client.actions.readGitDiff();
      await extensionFlush();
      if (field === "mode") {
        h.client.actions.selectGitMode("staged");
        h.client.actions.selectGitMode("base");
      } else {
        h.client.actions.selectGitBase("main");
        h.client.actions.selectGitBase("review/diverged-local-tip");
      }
      h.fixture.releaseGitReads();
      assert.equal(await pending, false);
      assert.equal(gitDetail(h), undefined);
      await extensionFlush();
      assert.equal(await h.client.actions.readGitDiff(), true);
    } finally {
      h.client.dispose();
    }
  });

for (const lane of ["status", "diff"])
  test(`Git synthetic ${lane} cancellation releases wire while late response cannot replace a newer read`, async () => {
    const h = setup();
    h.fixture.setGitState("ready");
    const status = await gitStatusWire(h);
    if (lane === "status") h.fixture.holdGitStatus = true;
    else h.fixture.holdGitDiff = true;
    const wire =
      lane === "status"
        ? gitWire(h)
        : gitWire(h, "git.diff.read", {
            observationId: status.value.observationId,
            mode: "unstaged",
            path: status.value.files[0].path,
          });
    const pending = h.transport.handle(h.event, wire);
    assert.equal(
      (
        await h.transport.handle(h.event, {
          ...wire,
          readId: wire.readId + "-other",
        })
      ).code,
      "READ_BUSY",
    );
    assert.equal(
      (
        await h.transport.cancel(h.event, {
          version: 2,
          runtimeEpoch: wire.runtimeEpoch,
          readId: wire.readId,
        })
      ).value.status,
      "cancelled",
    );
    assert.equal((await pending).code, "CANCELLED");
    h.fixture.releaseGitReads();
    const current = await h.transport.handle(h.event, {
      ...wire,
      readId: wire.readId + "-new",
    });
    assert.equal(current.ok, true);
    assert.equal(h.transport.metrics().active, 0);
    assert.equal(h.transport.mutationMetrics().attempted, 0);
  });

test("Git synthetic known stale detail preserves original OID and retires Renderer authority", async () => {
  const h = await gitController("expired");
  try {
    await h.client.actions.observeGitReview();
    gitSelect(h, "base", h.scope.path, "review/diverged-local-tip");
    await h.client.actions.readGitDiff();
    assert.equal(gitState(h).detail.reason, "expired_observation");
    assert.equal(gitState(h).detail.baseOid, "2".repeat(40));
    assert.equal(gitCurrent(h), undefined);
    assert.equal(gitDetail(h), undefined);
    assert.equal(gitCanDetail(h), false);
    assert.equal(gitState(h).error, "STALE");
  } finally {
    h.client.dispose();
  }
});

for (const [name, path, mode] of [
  ["ready", "notes/SYNTHETIC-untracked.txt", "unstaged"],
  ["unborn", "notes/SYNTHETIC-editor.txt", "staged"],
  ["ready", "notes/SYNTHETIC-other.txt", "unstaged"],
])
  test(`Git synthetic comparison bytes ${name}/${path}/${mode} preserve empty-side semantics`, async () => {
    const h = setup();
    h.fixture.setGitState(name);
    const detail = await gitDiffWire(h, await gitStatusWire(h), { path, mode });
    assert.equal(detail.value.status, "available");
    if (path === "notes/SYNTHETIC-other.txt")
      assert.equal(detail.value.patch, "");
    else {
      assert.match(detail.value.patch, /--- \/dev\/null\n/u);
      assert.match(detail.value.patch, /@@ -0,0 \+1 @@/u);
      assert.doesNotMatch(detail.value.patch, /^-SYNTHETIC (HEAD|INDEX)$/mu);
    }
  });

// Focused line feedback appendix. All original fixture tests above are retained.
const feedbackState = (h) => h.client.store.getState();
const feedbackDraft = (h) => h.selectors.lineFeedbackDraft(feedbackState(h));
const feedbackComposer = (h) => h.selectors.draft(feedbackState(h));
const feedbackReason = (h) => h.selectors.lineFeedbackReason(feedbackState(h));
function feedbackEffects(h) {
  return {
    reads: h.calls.filter((e) => e.readId).length,
    requests: h.fixture.requests.length,
    intents: h.fixture.intents.length,
    mutations: h.transport.mutationMetrics(),
    fileWrites: [...h.fixture.workspaceFileBackups],
    receipts: [...h.fixture.retainedReceipts],
  };
}
async function readyFileFeedback(name = "ready") {
  const h = await workspaceFileController(name);
  assert.equal(await h.client.actions.readWorkspaceFile(h.scope.path), true);
  assert.equal(h.client.actions.selectLineFeedbackSource("file"), true);
  h.client.actions.editLineFeedback({
    start: 1,
    end: 1,
    comment: "SYNTHETIC 의견",
  });
  return h;
}

test("Line feedback fixture holds actual Send ACK while newer local feedback survives", async () => {
  const h = await readyFileFeedback();
  try {
    const a = h.client.actions;
    a.draft("SYNTHETIC old submission");
    h.fixture.holdSendAck = true;
    const submitted = feedbackComposer(h);
    let settled = false;
    const pending = a
      .mutate(
        {
          type: "message.send",
          workspaceId: h.scope.workspaceId,
          sessionId: "fixture-s",
          requestId: "feedback-send",
          text: submitted.text,
        },
        false,
        submitted.text,
      )
      .then((result) => {
        settled = true;
        return result;
      });
    await fileFlush();
    assert.equal(
      h.fixture.requests.filter((r) => r.type === "message.send").length,
      1,
    );
    assert.equal(
      settled,
      false,
      "Synthetic transport must withhold actual ACK until explicit release",
    );
    assert.equal(h.fixture.heldSendAcks.length, 1);
    const expected = appendLineFeedback(
      submitted.text,
      formatLineFeedback(
        feedbackDraft(h).source,
        feedbackDraft(h).selection,
        feedbackDraft(h).comment,
      ).text,
    );
    assert.equal(
      a.appendLineFeedback(),
      true,
      `Pending explicit Send must permit local append; actual reason: ${feedbackReason(h)}`,
    );
    assert.equal(feedbackComposer(h).text, expected);
    assert.equal(feedbackComposer(h).revision, submitted.revision + 1);
    h.fixture.releaseSendAcks();
    await pending;
    assert.equal(settled, true);
    assert.equal(
      feedbackComposer(h).text,
      expected,
      "Old Send ACK cannot erase appended context",
    );
    assert.equal(h.fixture.requests.length, 1);
  } finally {
    h.fixture.releaseSendAcks?.();
    h.client.dispose();
  }
});

test("Line feedback actual owner appends original BOM CRLF baseline and preserves composer options without IO", async () => {
  const h = await readyFileFeedback();
  try {
    const a = h.client.actions;
    const original = feedbackDraft(h);
    const baseline = fileDraft(h).read;
    a.editWorkspaceFile("SYNTHETIC UNSAVED DIFFERENT TEXT\n");
    a.draft("SYNTHETIC existing composer");
    a.options({ agent: "fixture-agent", model: "fixture-model" });
    a.editLineFeedback({
      start: 1,
      end: 3,
      comment: 'SYNTHETIC "quoted" 의견\n<script>inert</script>',
    });
    const selected = feedbackDraft(h),
      before = feedbackComposer(h),
      effects = feedbackEffects(h);
    const expected = formatLineFeedback(
      selected.source,
      selected.selection,
      selected.comment,
    );
    assert.equal(selected.source.observation, baseline);
    assert.equal(original.source.observation, baseline);
    assert.equal(expected.excerpt, h.scope.text);
    assert.equal(expected.excerpt.startsWith("\ufeff"), true);
    assert.equal(expected.excerpt.includes("\r\n"), true);
    assert.equal(expected.reference.version, baseline.version);
    assert.equal(
      expected.reference.directoryVersion,
      baseline.directoryVersion,
    );
    assert.equal(a.appendLineFeedback(), true);
    assert.deepEqual(feedbackComposer(h), {
      ...before,
      text: appendLineFeedback(before.text, expected.text),
      revision: before.revision + 1,
    });
    assert.equal(feedbackComposer(h).text.includes("SYNTHETIC UNSAVED"), false);
    assert.deepEqual(feedbackEffects(h), effects);
    const appended = feedbackComposer(h);
    a.discardLineFeedback();
    assert.equal(feedbackDraft(h), undefined);
    assert.equal(feedbackComposer(h), appended);
    assert.deepEqual(feedbackEffects(h), effects);
  } finally {
    h.client.dispose();
  }
});

for (const mode of ["staged", "unstaged", "base"])
  for (const side of ["old", "new"])
    test(`Line feedback actual Git ${mode}/${side} binds original OID digest and exact no-newline coordinates`, async () => {
      const h = await gitController();
      try {
        h.fixture.gitPatchOverride = `diff --git a/${h.scope.path} b/${h.scope.path}\n--- a/${h.scope.path}\n+++ b/${h.scope.path}\n@@ -8 +12 @@\n-SYNTHETIC OLD\n\\ No newline at end of file\n+SYNTHETIC NEW\n\\ No newline at end of file\n`;
        assert.equal(await h.client.actions.observeGitReview(), true);
        gitSelect(h, mode, h.scope.path, "review/diverged-local-tip");
        assert.equal(await h.client.actions.readGitDiff(), true);
        const observation = gitDetail(h);
        assert.equal(h.client.actions.selectLineFeedbackSource("git"), true);
        const line = side === "old" ? 8 : 12;
        h.client.actions.editLineFeedback({
          side,
          start: line,
          end: line,
          comment: "SYNTHETIC Git 의견",
        });
        const before = feedbackEffects(h),
          d = feedbackDraft(h);
        const expected = formatLineFeedback(d.source, d.selection, d.comment);
        assert.equal(d.source.observation, observation);
        assert.equal(
          expected.excerpt,
          side === "old" ? "SYNTHETIC OLD" : "SYNTHETIC NEW",
        );
        assert.equal(expected.reference.sourceDigest, observation.sourceDigest);
        assert.equal(
          expected.reference.observationId,
          observation.observationId,
        );
        assert.equal(expected.reference.start, line);
        assert.equal(expected.reference.end, line);
        if (mode === "base") {
          assert.equal(expected.reference.baseOid, "2".repeat(40));
          assert.equal(expected.reference.baseRef, "review/diverged-local-tip");
        }
        assert.equal(h.client.actions.appendLineFeedback(), true);
        assert.equal(feedbackComposer(h).text, expected.text);
        assert.deepEqual(feedbackEffects(h), before);
      } finally {
        h.client.dispose();
      }
    });

for (const [name, patch, side, reason] of [
  ["empty", "", "new", "INVALID_LINE_RANGE"],
  [
    "absent-old",
    "diff --git a/x b/x\n--- /dev/null\n+++ b/x\n@@ -0,0 +1 @@\n+SYNTHETIC\n",
    "old",
    "INVALID_LINE_RANGE",
  ],
  [
    "absent-new",
    "diff --git a/x b/x\n--- a/x\n+++ /dev/null\n@@ -1 +0,0 @@\n-SYNTHETIC\n",
    "new",
    "INVALID_LINE_RANGE",
  ],
  [
    "malformed-count",
    "--- a/x\n+++ b/x\n@@ -1,2 +1 @@\n-SYNTHETIC\n+SYNTHETIC\n",
    "new",
    "UNSUPPORTED_DIFF",
  ],
  [
    "null-nonzero",
    "--- /dev/null\n+++ b/x\n@@ -1 +1 @@\n-SYNTHETIC\n+SYNTHETIC\n",
    "old",
    "UNSUPPORTED_DIFF",
  ],
  [
    "duplicate-header",
    "--- a/x\n--- a/x\n+++ b/x\n@@ -1 +1 @@\n-SYNTHETIC\n+SYNTHETIC\n",
    "new",
    "UNSUPPORTED_DIFF",
  ],
  [
    "one-sided-header",
    "--- a/x\n@@ -1 +1 @@\n-SYNTHETIC\n+SYNTHETIC\n",
    "new",
    "UNSUPPORTED_DIFF",
  ],
])
  test(`Line feedback corrected helper ${name} refuses through actual Git owner without effects`, async () => {
    const h = await gitController();
    try {
      h.fixture.gitPatchOverride = patch;
      await h.client.actions.observeGitReview();
      gitSelect(h);
      await h.client.actions.readGitDiff();
      assert.equal(h.client.actions.selectLineFeedbackSource("git"), true);
      h.client.actions.editLineFeedback({
        side,
        start: 1,
        end: 1,
        comment: "SYNTHETIC refused",
      });
      const before = feedbackEffects(h),
        composer = feedbackComposer(h),
        d = feedbackDraft(h);
      assert.equal(feedbackReason(h), reason);
      assert.equal(h.client.actions.appendLineFeedback(), false);
      assert.equal(feedbackComposer(h), composer);
      assert.equal(feedbackDraft(h), d);
      assert.deepEqual(feedbackEffects(h), before);
    } finally {
      h.client.dispose();
    }
  });

for (const name of ["blank", "protected", "missing"])
  test(`Line feedback ${name} file cannot fabricate selectable context`, async () => {
    const h = await workspaceFileController(name);
    try {
      await h.client.actions.readWorkspaceFile(h.scope.path);
      const available = name === "blank";
      assert.equal(
        h.client.actions.selectLineFeedbackSource("file"),
        available,
      );
      h.client.actions.editLineFeedback({ comment: "SYNTHETIC refused" });
      const effects = feedbackEffects(h);
      assert.equal(
        feedbackReason(h),
        available ? "INVALID_LINE_RANGE" : "MISSING_SOURCE",
      );
      assert.equal(h.client.actions.appendLineFeedback(), false);
      assert.equal(feedbackComposer(h).text, "");
      assert.deepEqual(feedbackEffects(h), effects);
    } finally {
      h.client.dispose();
    }
  });

for (const boundary of [
  "root",
  "epoch",
  "trust",
  "floor",
  "held",
  "global-held",
  "source-ABA",
  "session-ABA",
])
  test(`Line feedback ${boundary} retains editable inert original draft and explicit resume/discard`, async () => {
    const h = await readyFileFeedback();
    try {
      const a = h.client.actions,
        original = feedbackDraft(h),
        composer = feedbackComposer(h);
      if (boundary === "root") h.fixture.snapshot.workspaces[0].path += "-new";
      if (boundary === "epoch") h.fixture.snapshot.runtimeEpoch += "-new";
      if (boundary === "trust") {
        h.fixture.snapshot.workspaces[0].trusted = false;
        h.fixture.snapshot.workspaces[0].trustRevision++;
      }
      if (boundary === "floor") h.fixture.snapshot.revision++;
      if (boundary === "held")
        h.fixture.snapshot.recovery.resources = [
          {
            workspaceId: "fixture-w",
            state: "unavailable",
            reason: "unresolved_operation",
          },
        ];
      if (boundary === "global-held") {
        h.fixture.snapshot.recovery.store = {
          state: "recovery_required",
          reason: "corrupt_journal",
        };
        h.fixture.snapshot.state = "unavailable";
      }
      if (boundary === "source-ABA") {
        a.selectWorkspaceFile("notes/SYNTHETIC-other.txt");
        a.selectWorkspaceFile(h.scope.path);
      } else if (boundary === "session-ABA") {
        a.select("fixture-w");
        a.select("fixture-w", "fixture-s");
      }
      h.seed(boundary === "epoch");
      const before = feedbackEffects(h);
      a.editLineFeedback({ comment: "SYNTHETIC retained edit" });
      assert.equal(feedbackDraft(h).source, original.source);
      assert.equal(feedbackDraft(h).scope, original.scope);
      assert.equal(feedbackDraft(h).comment, "SYNTHETIC retained edit");
      assert.match(feedbackReason(h), /^(STALE_SOURCE|HELD|UNTRUSTED)$/);
      assert.equal(a.appendLineFeedback(), false);
      assert.equal(feedbackComposer(h).text, composer.text);
      a.resumeLineFeedback(original.id);
      assert.equal(feedbackDraft(h).source, original.source);
      a.discardLineFeedback();
      assert.equal(feedbackDraft(h), undefined);
      assert.equal(
        feedbackState(h).lineFeedback.drafts[original.id],
        undefined,
      );
      assert.deepEqual(feedbackEffects(h), before);
    } finally {
      h.client.dispose();
    }
  });

test("Line feedback source/session change never redirects a retained draft", async () => {
  const h = await readyFileFeedback();
  try {
    const a = h.client.actions,
      original = feedbackDraft(h);
    a.draft("SYNTHETIC original session composer");
    h.fixture.snapshot.sessions.push({
      id: "feedback-other-session",
      workspaceId: "fixture-w",
      title: "SYNTHETIC other session",
      status: "idle",
    });
    a.select("fixture-w", "feedback-other-session");
    h.fixture.snapshot.selectedSessionId = "feedback-other-session";
    h.seed();
    a.draft("SYNTHETIC other composer");
    await a.readWorkspaceFile(h.scope.path);
    a.selectLineFeedbackSource("file");
    const other = feedbackDraft(h);
    assert.notEqual(other.id, original.id);
    const before = feedbackEffects(h),
      composer = feedbackComposer(h);
    a.resumeLineFeedback(original.id);
    assert.equal(feedbackDraft(h).source, original.source);
    assert.equal(feedbackDraft(h).scope.sessionId, "fixture-s");
    assert.equal(a.appendLineFeedback(), false);
    assert.equal(feedbackComposer(h), composer);
    a.resumeLineFeedback(other.id);
    assert.equal(feedbackDraft(h).scope.sessionId, "feedback-other-session");
    assert.deepEqual(feedbackEffects(h), before);
  } finally {
    h.client.dispose();
  }
});

test("Line feedback overflow refuses without truncation or composer overwrite", async () => {
  const h = await readyFileFeedback();
  try {
    const a = h.client.actions,
      original = feedbackDraft(h);
    a.editLineFeedback({ comment: "x".repeat(2001) });
    assert.equal(feedbackDraft(h), original);
    assert.equal(feedbackState(h).lineFeedback.notice, "FEEDBACK_TOO_LARGE");
    a.draft("x".repeat(32000));
    const before = feedbackEffects(h),
      composer = feedbackComposer(h);
    assert.equal(feedbackReason(h), "DRAFT_TOO_LARGE");
    assert.equal(a.appendLineFeedback(), false);
    assert.equal(feedbackComposer(h), composer);
    assert.equal(feedbackDraft(h), original);
    assert.deepEqual(feedbackEffects(h), before);
  } finally {
    h.client.dispose();
  }
});

test("Line feedback selection range comment edit append discard poll refocus have no automatic file/Git/effect IO", async () => {
  const h = await readyFileFeedback();
  try {
    const a = h.client.actions;
    h.client.start();
    await extensionFlush();
    const reads = {
      file: h.fixture.workspaceFileReads.length,
      git: h.fixture.gitReads.length,
    };
    a.selectLineFeedbackSource("file");
    a.editLineFeedback({ start: 1, end: 2, comment: "SYNTHETIC local only" });
    a.editWorkspaceFile("SYNTHETIC local file edit");
    a.appendLineFeedback();
    a.discardLineFeedback();
    h.refocus();
    a.refresh();
    a.layout({}); // Theme/zoom are presentation only; actual DOM checks belong the saved runner.
    for (const [key, timer] of [...h.timers])
      if (timer.delay === 1200) {
        h.timers.delete(key);
        timer.callback();
      }
    await extensionFlush();
    assert.deepEqual(
      {
        file: h.fixture.workspaceFileReads.length,
        git: h.fixture.gitReads.length,
      },
      reads,
    );
    assert.equal(h.fixture.requests.length, 0);
    assert.equal(h.fixture.intents.length, 0);
    assert.deepEqual(h.transport.mutationMetrics(), {
      attempted: 0,
      dispatched: 0,
      rejected: 0,
    });
    assert.equal(h.fixture.workspaceFileBackups.size, 0);
  } finally {
    h.client.dispose();
  }
});

test("Line feedback synthetic ACK control delays only delivery, releases exact receipt and never repeats Send", async () => {
  const h = setup();
  h.fixture.holdSendAck = true;
  let settled = false;
  const pending = Promise.resolve(h.transport.handle(h.event, h.send)).then(
    (r) => {
      settled = true;
      return r;
    },
  );
  try {
    await fileFlush();
    assert.equal(settled, false);
    assert.equal(h.fixture.heldSendAcks.length, 1);
    assert.equal(h.fixture.requests.length, 1);
    const retained = [...h.fixture.retainedReceipts.values()][0].receipt;
    assert.equal(retained.delivery, "accepted");
    h.fixture.releaseSendAcks();
    const result = await pending;
    assert.equal(result.ok, true);
    assert.deepEqual(result.receipt, retained);
    h.fixture.releaseSendAcks();
    assert.equal(h.fixture.heldSendAcks.length, 0);
    assert.equal(h.fixture.requests.length, 1);
    assert.deepEqual(
      (await h.transport.handle(h.event, h.send)).receipt,
      retained,
    );
    assert.equal(h.fixture.requests.length, 1);
  } finally {
    h.fixture.releaseSendAcks();
    h.transport.close();
  }
});

for (const boundary of [
  "mode-ABA",
  "path-ABA",
  "base-ABA",
  "refresh",
  "root",
  "floor",
])
  test(`Line feedback Git ${boundary} cannot relabel an accepted old/new draft`, async () => {
    const h = await gitController();
    try {
      const a = h.client.actions;
      await a.observeGitReview();
      gitSelect(h, "base", h.scope.path, "review/diverged-local-tip");
      await a.readGitDiff();
      a.selectLineFeedbackSource("git");
      a.editLineFeedback({
        side: "old",
        start: 1,
        end: 1,
        comment: "SYNTHETIC original Git",
      });
      const original = feedbackDraft(h),
        observation = gitDetail(h);
      if (boundary === "mode-ABA") {
        a.selectGitMode("staged");
        a.selectGitMode("base");
      }
      if (boundary === "path-ABA") {
        a.selectGitPath(h.scope.otherPath);
        a.selectGitPath(h.scope.path);
      }
      if (boundary === "base-ABA") {
        a.selectGitBase("main");
        a.selectGitBase("review/diverged-local-tip");
      }
      if (boundary === "refresh") await a.observeGitReview();
      if (boundary === "root") {
        h.fixture.snapshot.workspaces[0].path += "-changed";
        h.seed();
      }
      if (boundary === "floor") {
        h.fixture.snapshot.revision++;
        h.seed();
      }
      const before = feedbackEffects(h);
      a.editLineFeedback({ comment: "SYNTHETIC still editable" });
      assert.equal(feedbackDraft(h).source.observation, observation);
      assert.equal(feedbackDraft(h).source, original.source);
      assert.equal(feedbackReason(h), "STALE_SOURCE");
      assert.equal(a.appendLineFeedback(), false);
      assert.equal(feedbackComposer(h).text, "");
      assert.deepEqual(feedbackEffects(h), before);
    } finally {
      h.client.dispose();
    }
  });

// Selected index fixtures are memory-only; no Git/native publication proof.
const indexContract = await import(
  "../../src/renderer/shared/git-index/index.mjs"
);
const indexStorage = await import(
  "../../src/host/workbench-git-index-storage.mjs"
);
async function indexPreview(
  h,
  action = "stage",
  paths = ["notes/SYNTHETIC-editor.txt"],
) {
  const observed = await gitStatusWire(h);
  assert.equal(observed.ok, true);
  return h.transport.handle(
    h.event,
    gitWire(h, "git.index.preview.read", {
      observationId: observed.value.observationId,
      action,
      paths,
    }),
  );
}
function indexApplyWire(h, preview, operationId = "synthetic-index-apply") {
  return {
    version: 2,
    runtimeEpoch: preview.runtimeEpoch,
    operationId,
    expectedRevision: preview.revision,
    operation: {
      type: "git.index.apply",
      workspaceId: preview.workspaceId,
      requestId: operationId,
      previewId: preview.previewId,
      ...Object.fromEntries(
        indexContract.GIT_INDEX_TARGET_FIELDS.map((k) => [k, preview[k]]),
      ),
    },
  };
}
for (const name of ["ready", "sha256", "absent", "unborn-existing"])
  for (const action of ["stage", "unstage"])
    test(`Index fixture ${name}/${action} binds exact target, original digest, floor and backup`, async () => {
      const h = setup();
      h.fixture.setGitIndexState(name);
      assert.equal(h.fixture.gitIndexPreviews.size, 0);
      assert.equal(h.fixture.gitIndexEffects.length, 0);
      const reply = await indexPreview(h, action);
      assert.equal(reply.ok, true);
      const preview = indexContract.validateGitIndexPreview(reply.value);
      if (name === "absent" && action === "unstage") {
        assert.equal(preview.reason, "no_changes");
        assert.equal(h.fixture.gitIndexEffects.length, 0);
        return;
      }
      assert.equal(preview.status, "available");
      assert.equal(preview.objectFormat, name === "sha256" ? "sha256" : "sha1");
      assert.equal(preview.indexExists, name !== "absent");
      const wire = indexApplyWire(h, preview);
      const admitted = {
        ...wire.operation,
        expectedRevision: wire.expectedRevision,
      };
      const originalDigest = createHash("sha256")
        .update(
          JSON.stringify(
            Object.keys(admitted)
              .sort()
              .map((k) => [k, admitted[k]]),
          ),
        )
        .digest("hex");
      const originalIndex = globalThis.structuredClone(
        h.fixture.syntheticGitIndexes.get(preview.workspaceId),
      );
      const result = await h.transport.handle(h.event, wire);
      assert.equal(result.ok, true);
      assert.equal(result.value.status, "applied");
      const target = indexContract.gitIndexTargetFromApply(wire.operation);
      assert.equal(
        indexStorage.validGitIndexUpdatedResult(
          result.receipt,
          target,
          originalDigest,
        ),
        true,
      );
      assert.equal(
        indexStorage.validGitIndexUpdatedResult(
          result.receipt,
          target,
          "f".repeat(64),
        ),
        false,
      );
      assert.equal(
        result.value.backupId,
        name === "absent"
          ? null
          : indexStorage.gitIndexBackupId(wire.runtimeEpoch, wire.operationId),
      );
      if (preview.indexExists)
        assert.deepEqual(
          h.fixture.syntheticGitIndexBackups.get(result.value.backupId),
          originalIndex,
        );
      else assert.equal(h.fixture.syntheticGitIndexBackups.size, 0);
      assert.equal(h.fixture.gitIndexEffects.length, 1);
      const updated = h.fixture.syntheticGitIndexes.get(preview.workspaceId);
      assert.deepEqual(
        updated.entries["notes/SYNTHETIC-other.txt"],
        originalIndex.entries["notes/SYNTHETIC-other.txt"],
      );
      assert.deepEqual(
        updated.entries[preview.paths[0]] ?? null,
        preview.entries[0].after,
      );
      const duplicate = await h.transport.handle(h.event, wire);
      assert.equal(duplicate.value.status, "duplicate");
      assert.equal(h.fixture.gitIndexEffects.length, 1);
      assert.equal(
        (
          await h.transport.handle(h.event, {
            ...wire,
            expectedRevision: wire.expectedRevision + 1,
          })
        ).code,
        "DUPLICATE_CONFLICT",
      );
      assert.equal(
        h.fixture.gitIndexReads.length,
        1,
        "Completion does not auto-preview/reread",
      );
      const stale = await h.transport.handle(h.event, {
        ...wire,
        operationId: "another",
        operation: { ...wire.operation, requestId: "another" },
      });
      assert.equal(stale.code, "STALE_REVISION");
      const reread = await indexPreview(h, action);
      assert.equal(reread.value.revision, result.receipt.revision);
    });

for (const reason of [
  "no_changes",
  "stale_observation",
  "expired_observation",
  "lock_conflict",
  "unsupported_index_mutation",
])
  test(`Index fixture preview refusal ${reason} has no effect`, async () => {
    const h = setup();
    h.fixture.setGitIndexState(reason);
    const result = await indexPreview(h);
    assert.equal(result.ok, true);
    assert.equal(result.value.reason, reason);
    indexContract.validateGitIndexPreview(result.value);
    assert.equal(h.fixture.gitIndexEffects.length, 0);
  });

for (const outcome of [
  "denied",
  "unknown",
  "stale_preview",
  "expired_preview",
  "lock_conflict",
  "unsupported_index_mutation",
])
  test(`Index fixture Apply ${outcome} is explicit and never replayed`, async () => {
    const h = setup();
    h.fixture.setGitIndexState("ready");
    const preview = (await indexPreview(h)).value;
    h.fixture.nextGitIndexApply = outcome;
    const result = await h.transport.handle(
      h.event,
      indexApplyWire(h, preview),
    );
    if (outcome === "unknown") {
      assert.equal(result.value.status, "unknown");
      assert.equal(result.receipt.effect, "unknown");
    } else {
      assert.equal(result.ok, false);
      assert.equal(result.receipt.effect, "none");
    }
    assert.equal(h.fixture.gitIndexEffects.length, 0);
    assert.equal(h.fixture.syntheticGitIndexBackups.size, 0);
    indexStorage.validateGitIndexReceipt(result.receipt);
  });

test("Index fixture exact closed target and sender preview binding refuse forgery", async () => {
  const h = setup();
  h.fixture.setGitIndexState("ready");
  const preview = (await indexPreview(h)).value;
  for (const field of indexContract.GIT_INDEX_TARGET_FIELDS) {
    const wire = indexApplyWire(h, preview, `forged-${field}`);
    wire.operation[field] =
      field === "paths"
        ? ["notes/SYNTHETIC-other.txt"]
        : field === "action"
          ? "unstage"
          : field === "indexExists"
            ? false
            : field === "objectFormat"
              ? "sha256"
              : "f".repeat(field === "headOid" ? 40 : 64);
    const result = await h.transport.handle(h.event, wire);
    assert.equal(result.ok, false, field);
  }
  const wire = indexApplyWire(h, preview);
  assert.equal(
    (
      await h.transport.handle(h.event, {
        ...wire,
        operation: { ...wire.operation, extra: true },
      })
    ).code,
    "INVALID_REQUEST",
  );
  assert.equal(h.fixture.gitIndexEffects.length, 0);
});

test("Index fixture held actual call stays busy through confirmed lookup until finally release", async () => {
  const h = setup();
  h.fixture.setGitIndexState("ready");
  const preview = (await indexPreview(h)).value;
  const wire = indexApplyWire(h, preview);
  h.fixture.holdGitIndexApply = true;
  let settled = false;
  const call = h.transport.handle(h.event, wire).then((r) => {
    settled = true;
    return r;
  });
  h.fixture.confirmGitIndexApplies();
  const lookup = await h.transport.handle(h.event, {
    ...gitWire(h),
    operation: {
      type: "operation.lookup",
      runtimeEpoch: wire.runtimeEpoch,
      operationId: wire.operationId,
    },
  });
  assert.equal(lookup.value.receipt.effect, "confirmed");
  assert.equal(settled, false);
  assert.equal(
    h.fixture.pendingGitIndexWorkspaces.has(preview.workspaceId),
    true,
  );
  assert.equal((await gitStatusWire(h)).code, "UNAVAILABLE");
  const other = h.fixture.snapshot.workspaces.find(
    (w) => w.id !== preview.workspaceId,
  );
  assert.ok(other);
  const independent = await h.transport.handle(
    h.event,
    gitWire(h, "git.review.read", { workspaceId: other.id }),
  );
  assert.equal(independent.ok, true);
  h.fixture.releaseGitIndexApplies();
  assert.equal((await call).value.status, "applied");
  assert.equal(h.fixture.pendingGitIndexWorkspaces.size, 0);
});

test("Index fixture retained receipt keeps original epoch and operation key", async () => {
  const h = setup();
  h.fixture.setGitIndexState("retained");
  const key = h.fixture.gitIndexRetainedKey;
  assert.notEqual(key.runtimeEpoch, h.fixture.snapshot.runtimeEpoch);
  const found = await h.transport.handle(h.event, {
    ...gitWire(h),
    operation: { type: "operation.lookup", ...key },
  });
  assert.equal(found.value.status, "found");
  assert.equal(found.value.receipt.effect, "unknown");
  assert.equal(found.value.receipt.runtimeEpoch, key.runtimeEpoch);
  assert.equal(h.fixture.gitIndexEffects.length, 0);
});

async function indexController(name = "ready") {
  const h = setup();
  const scope = h.fixture.setGitIndexState(name);
  return {
    ...(await extensionController("populated", { existing: h })),
    scope,
  };
}
const indexState = (h) => h.selectors.gitIndex(h.client.store.getState());
const indexCurrent = (h) =>
  h.selectors.currentGitIndexPreview(h.client.store.getState());
async function controllerIndexPreview(h) {
  assert.equal(await h.client.actions.observeGitReview(), true);
  h.client.actions.toggleGitIndexPath(h.scope.path);
  assert.equal(await h.client.actions.previewGitIndex(), true);
  assert.equal(indexCurrent(h).status, "available");
}

test("Index actual controller preview/apply requires explicit reads and a new snapshot floor", async () => {
  const h = await indexController();
  try {
    h.client.start();
    await extensionFlush();
    h.refocus();
    h.client.actions.refresh();
    h.client.actions.selectGitIndexAction("unstage");
    h.client.actions.editConfigurationDraft(
      "instructions",
      "SYNTHETIC preserved draft",
    );
    await extensionFlush();
    assert.equal(h.fixture.gitReads.length, 0);
    assert.equal(h.fixture.gitIndexReads.length, 0);
    await controllerIndexPreview(h);
    const applied = await h.client.actions.applyGitIndex();
    assert.equal(applied, true);
    assert.equal(indexState(h).mutation.status, "applied");
    assert.equal(indexCurrent(h), undefined);
    assert.equal(h.fixture.gitIndexReads.length, 1);
    assert.equal(h.fixture.gitReads.length, 1);
    assert.equal(await h.client.actions.applyGitIndex(), false);
    h.seed();
    assert.equal(await h.client.actions.observeGitReview(), true);
    assert.equal(h.fixture.gitReads.length, 2);
    assert.equal(h.fixture.gitIndexEffects.length, 1);
  } finally {
    h.client.dispose();
  }
});

test("Index actual controller cancelled read retains underlying IO lease until release", async () => {
  const h = await indexController("pending-preview");
  try {
    assert.equal(await h.client.actions.observeGitReview(), true);
    h.client.actions.toggleGitIndexPath(h.scope.path);
    const pending = h.client.actions.previewGitIndex();
    await extensionFlush();
    h.client.actions.cancelGitIndexPreview();
    assert.equal(await pending, false);
    assert.equal(indexCurrent(h), undefined);
    assert.equal(h.fixture.pendingGitIndexReadWorkspaces.size, 1);
    assert.equal(await h.client.actions.previewGitIndex(), false);
    h.fixture.releaseGitIndexReads();
    await extensionFlush();
    assert.equal(h.transport.metrics().active, 0);
    assert.equal(
      indexCurrent(h),
      undefined,
      "Late read cannot restore cancelled preview",
    );
    assert.equal(await h.client.actions.previewGitIndex(), true);
  } finally {
    h.fixture.releaseGitIndexReads();
    h.client.dispose();
  }
});

test("Index actual controller late Apply ACK cannot select original workspace or erase other draft", async () => {
  const h = await indexController();
  try {
    await controllerIndexPreview(h);
    h.fixture.holdGitIndexApply = true;
    const pending = h.client.actions.applyGitIndex();
    for (let n = 0; n < 100 && h.fixture.heldGitIndexApplies.length === 0; n++)
      await new Promise((resolve) => setTimeout(resolve, 2));
    assert.equal(h.fixture.heldGitIndexApplies.length, 1);
    h.client.actions.select(
      "synthetic-index-other-w",
      "synthetic-index-other-s",
    );
    h.client.actions.editConfigurationDraft(
      "instructions",
      "SYNTHETIC other workspace draft",
    );
    h.fixture.confirmGitIndexApplies();
    h.seed();
    await extensionFlush();
    h.fixture.releaseGitIndexApplies();
    await pending;
    await extensionFlush();
    assert.equal(
      h.client.store.getState().selection.workspaceId,
      "synthetic-index-other-w",
    );
    assert.equal(
      h.selectors.configurationDraft("instructions")(h.client.store.getState())
        .text,
      "SYNTHETIC other workspace draft",
    );
    assert.equal(h.fixture.gitIndexEffects.length, 1);
    assert.equal(
      h.fixture.requests.filter((r) => r.type === "git.index.apply").length,
      1,
    );
    h.client.actions.select("fixture-w", "fixture-s");
    h.client.actions.refresh();
    h.refocus();
    await extensionFlush();
    assert.equal(h.fixture.gitIndexReads.length, 1);
    assert.equal(h.fixture.gitIndexEffects.length, 1);
  } finally {
    h.fixture.releaseGitIndexApplies();
    h.client.dispose();
  }
});

test("Index actual controller unknown and retained intents use original key lookup without replay", async () => {
  for (const name of ["unknown", "retained"]) {
    const h = await indexController(name);
    try {
      if (name === "unknown") {
        await controllerIndexPreview(h);
        assert.equal(await h.client.actions.applyGitIndex(), false);
        assert.equal(indexState(h).mutation.status, "unknown");
      }
      h.client.start();
      h.client.actions.refresh();
      h.refocus();
      await extensionFlush();
      for (const [id, timer] of [...h.timers])
        if ([400, 5000].includes(timer.delay)) {
          h.timers.delete(id);
          timer.callback();
        }
      await extensionFlush();
      assert.ok(h.fixture.lookups.length > 0);
      if (name === "retained")
        assert.ok(
          h.fixture.lookups.every(
            (r) =>
              r.runtimeEpoch === h.fixture.gitIndexRetainedKey.runtimeEpoch &&
              r.operationId === h.fixture.gitIndexRetainedKey.operationId,
          ),
        );
      assert.equal(
        h.fixture.requests.filter((r) => r.type === "git.index.apply").length,
        name === "unknown" ? 1 : 0,
      );
      assert.equal(h.fixture.gitIndexEffects.length, 0);
    } finally {
      h.client.dispose();
    }
  }
});

test("Index fixture pending preview reset cannot revive an earlier generation", async () => {
  const h = setup();
  h.fixture.setGitIndexState("pending-preview");
  const pending = indexPreview(h);
  await extensionFlush();
  assert.equal(h.fixture.heldGitIndexReads.length, 1);
  h.fixture.setGitIndexState("ready");
  h.fixture.releaseGitIndexReads();
  assert.equal((await pending).code, "STALE");
  assert.equal(h.fixture.gitIndexEffects.length, 0);
});

test("Index fixture preview binds sender and actual elapsed expiry", async () => {
  const h = setup();
  h.fixture.setGitIndexState("ready");
  const preview = (await indexPreview(h)).value;
  const second = createFixtureTransport(h.fixture, {
    webContents: h.event.sender,
    allowedUrl: () => true,
  });
  const forged = await second.handle(
    h.event,
    indexApplyWire(h, preview, "foreign-sender"),
  );
  assert.equal(forged.code, "FILE_CHANGED");
  const fresh = (await indexPreview(h)).value;
  h.fixture.gitNow += indexContract.GIT_INDEX_LIMITS.previewMs;
  assert.equal(
    (
      await h.transport.handle(
        h.event,
        indexApplyWire(h, fresh, "expired-call"),
      )
    ).code,
    "FILE_CHANGED",
  );
  const observed = await gitStatusWire(h);
  h.fixture.gitNow += gitLimits.observationMs;
  const expired = await h.transport.handle(
    h.event,
    gitWire(h, "git.index.preview.read", {
      observationId: observed.value.observationId,
      action: "stage",
      paths: ["notes/SYNTHETIC-editor.txt"],
    }),
  );
  assert.equal(expired.value.reason, "expired_observation");
  assert.equal(h.fixture.gitIndexEffects.length, 0);
});

test("Index runner capture retains exclusive PNGs and refuses focus or incomplete surfaces", async (t) => {
  const source = await readFile(
    new URL("../e2e-git-index.mjs", import.meta.url),
    "utf8",
  );
  const captureSource = source.match(
    /async function capture\(page, name, state\) \{[\s\S]*?\n\}/u,
  )?.[0];
  assert.ok(captureSource);
  const root = await realpath(
    await mkdtemp(join(tmpdir(), "omoc-index-capture-")),
  );
  t.after(() => rm(root, { recursive: true, force: true }));
  const png = Buffer.alloc(24);
  Buffer.from("89504e470d0a1a0a", "hex").copy(png);
  png.writeUInt32BE(100, 16);
  png.writeUInt32BE(100, 20);
  const native = {
    png: png.toString("base64"),
    before: { visible: false, focused: false, focusable: false },
    osWindowVisible: false,
    osWindowFocused: false,
    osWindowFocusable: false,
    contentBounds: { width: 100, height: 100 },
    windowBounds: { width: 100, height: 100 },
    displayScaleFactor: 1,
    zoomFactor: 1,
  };
  const screenshots = [];
  const capture = new Function(
    "application",
    "assert",
    "join",
    "run",
    "writeFile",
    "screenshots",
    `return (${captureSource});`,
  )(
    { evaluate: async () => native },
    assert,
    join,
    root,
    writeFile,
    screenshots,
  );
  const page = {
    evaluate: async () => ({ width: 100, height: 100, pixelRatio: 1 }),
  };
  await capture(page, "first.png", "first");
  await assert.rejects(capture(page, "first.png", "duplicate"), {
    code: "EEXIST",
  });
  assert.deepEqual(screenshots[0].afterCapture, {
    visible: false,
    focused: false,
    focusable: false,
  });
  native.osWindowFocusable = true;
  await assert.rejects(capture(page, "focus.png", "focus"));
  native.osWindowFocusable = false;
  native.contentBounds.width = 1010;
  await assert.rejects(capture(page, "clipped.png", "clipped"));
  assert.equal(screenshots.length, 1);
});

// Explicit Commit: memory-only host + actual closed helpers/controller.
const commitContract = await import(
  "../../src/renderer/shared/git-commit/index.mjs"
);
const commitStorage = await import(
  "../../src/host/workbench-git-commit-storage.mjs"
);
const commitPrivate = await import("../../src/host/git-commit-contract.mjs");
const syntheticAuthor = {
  name: "SYNTHETIC 작성자",
  email: "synthetic@example.invalid",
};
const syntheticMessage =
  "\ufeffSYNTHETIC <script>inert</script>\r\nsecond line\r\n";
async function commitPreviewWire(h, extra = {}) {
  const observed = await gitStatusWire(h);
  assert.equal(observed.ok, true);
  return h.transport.handle(
    h.event,
    gitWire(h, "git.commit.preview.read", {
      observationId: observed.value.observationId,
      author: syntheticAuthor,
      message: syntheticMessage,
      ...extra,
    }),
  );
}
function commitApplyWire(h, p, operationId = "synthetic-commit-apply") {
  return {
    version: 2,
    runtimeEpoch: p.runtimeEpoch,
    operationId,
    expectedRevision: p.revision,
    operation: {
      type: "git.commit.apply",
      workspaceId: p.workspaceId,
      requestId: operationId,
      previewId: p.previewId,
      ...Object.fromEntries(
        commitContract.GIT_COMMIT_TARGET_FIELDS.map((k) => [k, p[k]]),
      ),
    },
  };
}
for (const name of [
  "ready",
  "sha256",
  "unborn",
  "detached",
  "packed",
  "disabled-existing-logs",
  "absent-logs",
  "full-staged-deletion",
]) {
  test(
    "Commit fixture exact closed target/digest/backups/all-staged: " + name,
    async () => {
      const h = setup();
      h.fixture.setGitCommitState(name);
      const p = (await commitPreviewWire(h)).value;
      commitContract.validateGitCommitPreview(p);
      assert.equal(p.status, "available");
      assert.equal(p.entries.length, 2);
      assert.equal(p.canonicalMessage, syntheticMessage);
      const originalIndex = globalThis.structuredClone(
        h.fixture.syntheticGitIndexes,
      );
      const envelope = commitApplyWire(h, p);
      const response = await h.transport.handle(h.event, envelope);
      assert.equal(response.ok, true);
      assert.equal(response.value.status, "committed");
      const target = commitContract.gitCommitTargetFromApply(
        envelope.operation,
      );
      const expectedDigest = createHash("sha256")
        .update(
          JSON.stringify(
            Object.entries({
              ...envelope.operation,
              expectedRevision: p.revision,
            }).sort(([a], [b]) => (a < b ? -1 : a > b ? 1 : 0)),
          ),
        )
        .digest("hex");
      assert.equal(response.receipt.result.digest, expectedDigest);
      assert.ok(
        commitStorage.validGitCommitCreatedResult(
          response.receipt,
          target,
          expectedDigest,
        ),
      );
      assert.deepEqual(
        response.receipt.result.backups,
        commitPrivate.gitCommitExpectedBackups(
          target,
          p.runtimeEpoch,
          envelope.operationId,
        ),
      );
      assert.equal(
        JSON.stringify(response.receipt).includes(syntheticMessage),
        false,
      );
      assert.deepEqual(h.fixture.syntheticGitIndexes, originalIndex);
      assert.equal(h.fixture.gitCommitEffects.length, 1);
      if (name === "sha256") assert.equal(p.commitOid.length, 64);
      if (name === "unborn") assert.equal(p.headOid, null);
      if (name === "detached") assert.equal(p.reference.kind, "head");
      if (["packed", "unborn"].includes(name))
        assert.equal(response.receipt.result.backups.at(-1).backupId, null);
      if (name === "absent-logs") assert.deepEqual(p.logs, []);
      if (name === "full-staged-deletion")
        assert.ok(p.entries.every((e) => e.after === null));
      const duplicate = await h.transport.handle(h.event, envelope);
      assert.equal(duplicate.value.status, "duplicate");
      assert.equal(h.fixture.gitCommitEffects.length, 1);
    },
  );
}

for (const reason of ["no_changes", "unsupported_commit_state"])
  test("Commit fixture refusal " + reason, async () => {
    const h = setup();
    h.fixture.setGitCommitState(reason);
    const r = await commitPreviewWire(h);
    assert.equal(r.value.status, "unavailable");
    assert.equal(r.value.reason, reason);
    assert.equal(h.fixture.gitCommitEffects.length, 0);
  });
for (const outcome of [
  "denied",
  "unknown",
  "expired_preview",
  "stale_preview",
  "lock_conflict",
  "unsupported_commit_state",
])
  test("Commit fixture apply refusal/unknown " + outcome, async () => {
    const h = setup();
    h.fixture.setGitCommitState("ready");
    const p = (await commitPreviewWire(h)).value;
    h.fixture.nextGitCommitApply = outcome;
    const r = await h.transport.handle(h.event, commitApplyWire(h, p));
    assert.equal(r.ok, outcome === "unknown");
    assert.equal(r.receipt.effect, outcome === "unknown" ? "unknown" : "none");
    commitStorage.validateGitCommitReceipt(r.receipt);
    assert.equal(h.fixture.gitCommitEffects.length, 0);
    assert.equal(h.fixture.syntheticGitCommitBackups.size, 0);
    if (outcome === "unknown") {
      h.fixture.nextGitCommitApply = undefined;
      const dup = await h.transport.handle(h.event, commitApplyWire(h, p));
      assert.equal(dup.value.status, "duplicate");
      assert.equal(dup.receipt.effect, "unknown");
    }
  });
test("Commit fixture exact input closure, sender, expiry, marker and target guards", async () => {
  for (const corruption of [
    "extra",
    "identity",
    "sender",
    "expiry",
    "target",
    "marker",
    "requestId",
    "revision",
  ]) {
    const h = setup();
    h.fixture.setGitCommitState("ready");
    const p = (await commitPreviewWire(h)).value;
    const e = commitApplyWire(h, p);
    let transport = h.transport;
    if (corruption === "extra") e.operation.author = syntheticAuthor;
    if (corruption === "identity") {
      const r = await commitPreviewWire(h, {
        author: { ...syntheticAuthor, name: " SYNTHETIC" },
      });
      assert.equal(r.ok, false);
      continue;
    }
    if (corruption === "sender")
      transport = createFixtureTransport(h.fixture, {
        webContents: h.event.sender,
        allowedUrl: () => true,
      });
    if (corruption === "expiry")
      h.fixture.gitNow += commitContract.GIT_COMMIT_LIMITS.previewMs;
    if (corruption === "target") e.operation.commitOid = "9".repeat(40);
    if (corruption === "marker")
      h.fixture.syntheticGitCommits.get(p.workspaceId).marker = true;
    if (corruption === "requestId") e.operation.requestId = "other";
    if (corruption === "revision") e.expectedRevision++;
    assert.equal((await transport.handle(h.event, e)).ok, false, corruption);
    assert.equal(h.fixture.gitCommitEffects.length, 0);
  }
});
async function commitController(name = "ready") {
  const h = setup(),
    scope = h.fixture.setGitCommitState(name);
  return {
    ...(await extensionController("populated", { existing: h })),
    scope,
  };
}
const commitCurrent = (h) =>
  h.selectors.currentGitCommitPreview(h.client.store.getState());
const commitDraft = (h) =>
  h.selectors.gitCommitDraft(h.client.store.getState());
async function controllerCommitPreview(h) {
  assert.equal(await h.client.actions.observeGitReview(), true);
  h.client.actions.editGitCommit({
    ...syntheticAuthor,
    message: syntheticMessage,
  });
  assert.equal(await h.client.actions.previewGitCommit(), true);
  assert.equal(commitCurrent(h).status, "available");
}
test("Commit actual controller explicit all-staged, canonical message, success floor and no autoIO", async () => {
  const h = await commitController();
  try {
    assert.deepEqual(
      { ...commitDraft(h) },
      { name: "", email: "", message: "", revision: 0 },
    );
    h.client.start();
    await extensionFlush();
    h.refocus();
    h.client.actions.refresh();
    await extensionFlush();
    h.client.actions.editGitCommit({
      ...syntheticAuthor,
      message: syntheticMessage,
    });
    h.client.actions.toggleGitIndexPath(h.scope.path);
    assert.equal(h.fixture.gitReads.length, 0);
    assert.equal(h.fixture.gitCommitReads.length, 0);
    await controllerCommitPreview(h);
    assert.equal(commitCurrent(h).entries.length, 2);
    assert.equal(commitCurrent(h).canonicalMessage, syntheticMessage);
    assert.equal(await h.client.actions.applyGitCommit(), true);
    assert.equal(commitCurrent(h), undefined);
    assert.equal(await h.client.actions.applyGitCommit(), false);
    assert.equal(h.fixture.gitReads.length, 1);
    assert.equal(h.fixture.gitCommitReads.length, 1);
    assert.equal(
      h.fixture.requests.filter((r) => r.type === "git.index.apply").length,
      0,
    );
    h.seed();
    assert.equal(await h.client.actions.observeGitReview(), true);
    assert.equal(h.fixture.gitReads.length, 2);
    assert.equal(h.fixture.gitCommitEffects.length, 1);
  } finally {
    h.client.dispose();
  }
});
test("Commit actual controller cancellation keeps underlying preview lane until actual finally", async () => {
  const h = await commitController("pending-preview");
  try {
    await h.client.actions.observeGitReview();
    h.client.actions.editGitCommit({
      ...syntheticAuthor,
      message: syntheticMessage,
    });
    const pending = h.client.actions.previewGitCommit();
    await extensionFlush();
    h.client.actions.cancelGitCommitPreview();
    assert.equal(await pending, false);
    assert.equal(h.fixture.pendingGitCommitReadWorkspaces.size, 1);
    assert.equal(await h.client.actions.previewGitCommit(), false);
    h.fixture.releaseGitCommitReads();
    await extensionFlush();
    assert.equal(commitCurrent(h), undefined);
    assert.equal(h.fixture.pendingGitCommitReadWorkspaces.size, 0);
    assert.equal(await h.client.actions.previewGitCommit(), true);
  } finally {
    h.fixture.releaseGitCommitReads();
    h.client.dispose();
  }
});
for (const boundary of [
  "held",
  "untrusted",
  "root",
  "epoch",
  "workspace",
  "newer-draft",
])
  test(
    "Commit actual controller retained editable historical draft: " + boundary,
    async () => {
      const h = await commitController();
      try {
        await controllerCommitPreview(h);
        const oldKey = h.selectors.currentGitCommitDraftKey(
          h.client.store.getState(),
        );
        if (boundary === "held")
          h.fixture.snapshot.recovery.resources.push({
            workspaceId: "fixture-w",
            state: "unavailable",
            reason: "unresolved_operation",
          });
        if (boundary === "untrusted") {
          h.fixture.snapshot.workspaces[0].trusted = false;
          h.fixture.snapshot.workspaces[0].trustRevision++;
        }
        if (boundary === "root")
          h.fixture.snapshot.workspaces[0].path =
            "/synthetic/commit-replaced-root";
        if (boundary === "epoch")
          h.fixture.snapshot.runtimeEpoch =
            "synthetic-replacement-commit-epoch";
        if (boundary === "workspace")
          h.client.actions.select(
            "synthetic-index-other-w",
            "synthetic-index-other-s",
          );
        if (!["workspace", "newer-draft"].includes(boundary)) {
          h.fixture.snapshot.revision++;
          h.seed();
        }
        if (["root", "epoch", "workspace"].includes(boundary))
          h.client.actions.selectGitCommitDraft(oldKey);
        h.client.actions.editGitCommit({ message: "SYNTHETIC still editable" });
        assert.equal(commitDraft(h).message, "SYNTHETIC still editable");
        assert.equal(commitCurrent(h), undefined);
        assert.equal(await h.client.actions.applyGitCommit(), false);
        assert.equal(h.fixture.gitCommitEffects.length, 0);
      } finally {
        h.client.dispose();
      }
    },
  );
for (const name of ["unknown", "retained"])
  test(
    "Commit actual controller original-key current-envelope lookup and no replay: " +
      name,
    async () => {
      const h = await commitController(name);
      try {
        if (name === "unknown") {
          await controllerCommitPreview(h);
          assert.equal(await h.client.actions.applyGitCommit(), false);
        }
        h.client.start();
        h.client.actions.refresh();
        h.refocus();
        await extensionFlush();
        for (const [id, timer] of [...h.timers])
          if ([400, 5000].includes(timer.delay)) {
            h.timers.delete(id);
            timer.callback();
          }
        await extensionFlush();
        assert.ok(h.fixture.lookups.length > 0);
        const key =
          name === "retained"
            ? h.fixture.gitCommitRetainedKey
            : h.fixture.intents.at(-1);
        assert.ok(
          h.fixture.lookupEnvelopes.every(
            (e) =>
              e.runtimeEpoch === h.fixture.snapshot.runtimeEpoch &&
              e.operation.runtimeEpoch === key.runtimeEpoch &&
              e.operation.operationId === key.operationId,
          ),
        );
        h.client.actions.editGitCommit({ message: "SYNTHETIC held editable" });
        assert.equal(commitDraft(h).message, "SYNTHETIC held editable");
        assert.equal(
          h.fixture.requests.filter((r) => r.type === "git.commit.apply")
            .length,
          name === "unknown" ? 1 : 0,
        );
        assert.equal(h.fixture.gitCommitEffects.length, 0);
      } finally {
        h.client.dispose();
      }
    },
  );
for (const boundary of ["newer-draft", "workspace", "root", "epoch"])
  test("Commit actual controller late ACK preserves " + boundary, async () => {
    const h = await commitController();
    try {
      await controllerCommitPreview(h);
      h.fixture.holdGitCommitApply = true;
      const pending = h.client.actions.applyGitCommit();
      for (let n = 0; n < 100 && !h.fixture.heldGitCommitApplies.length; n++)
        await new Promise((resolve) => setTimeout(resolve, 2));
      assert.equal(h.fixture.heldGitCommitApplies.length, 1);
      // Receipt may settle before the original invocation. Keep both holds.
      h.fixture.confirmGitCommitApplies();
      h.seed();
      await extensionFlush();
      assert.equal(h.fixture.pendingGitCommitWorkspaces.size, 1);
      assert.equal(
        Object.keys(h.client.store.getState().gitCommitIO).length,
        1,
      );
      assert.equal(await h.client.actions.observeGitReview(), false);
      if (boundary === "workspace")
        h.client.actions.select(
          "synthetic-index-other-w",
          "synthetic-index-other-s",
        );
      if (boundary === "root")
        h.fixture.snapshot.workspaces[0].path = "/synthetic/late-commit-root";
      if (boundary === "epoch")
        h.fixture.snapshot.runtimeEpoch = "synthetic-late-commit-epoch";
      if (["root", "epoch"].includes(boundary)) {
        h.fixture.snapshot.revision++;
        h.seed();
      }
      h.client.actions.editGitCommit({
        ...syntheticAuthor,
        message: "SYNTHETIC newer draft",
      });
      h.fixture.releaseGitCommitApplies();
      await pending;
      await extensionFlush();
      assert.equal(h.fixture.pendingGitCommitWorkspaces.size, 0);
      assert.equal(
        Object.keys(h.client.store.getState().gitCommitIO).length,
        0,
      );
      assert.equal(commitDraft(h).message, "SYNTHETIC newer draft");
      assert.equal(h.fixture.gitCommitEffects.length, 1);
      assert.equal(
        h.fixture.requests.filter((r) => r.type === "git.commit.apply").length,
        1,
      );
    } finally {
      h.fixture.releaseGitCommitApplies();
      h.client.dispose();
    }
  });
test("Commit fixture completed receipt cannot supply forged result authority", async () => {
  const h = setup();
  h.fixture.setGitCommitState("ready");
  const p = (await commitPreviewWire(h)).value;
  const e = commitApplyWire(h, p),
    r = await h.transport.handle(h.event, e),
    target = commitContract.gitCommitTargetFromApply(e.operation);
  for (const field of [
    "digest",
    "commitOid",
    "treeOid",
    "parentOid",
    "previewDigest",
    "referenceDigest",
  ]) {
    const forged = globalThis.structuredClone(r.receipt);
    forged.result[field] = "f".repeat(forged.result[field].length);
    assert.equal(
      commitStorage.validGitCommitCreatedResult(
        forged,
        target,
        r.receipt.result.digest,
      ),
      false,
      field,
    );
  }
  const forged = globalThis.structuredClone(r.receipt);
  forged.result.backups[0].backupId = commitPrivate.gitCommitExpectedBackups(
    target,
    "foreign-epoch",
    "foreign-op",
  )[0].backupId;
  assert.equal(
    commitStorage.validGitCommitCreatedResult(
      forged,
      target,
      r.receipt.result.digest,
    ),
    false,
  );
});

test("Commit runner capture retains exclusive PNGs and refuses focus or incomplete surfaces", async (t) => {
  const source = await readFile(
    new URL("../e2e-git-commit.mjs", import.meta.url),
    "utf8",
  );
  const captureSource = source.match(
    /async function capture\(page, name, state\) \{[\s\S]*?\n\}/u,
  )?.[0];
  assert.ok(captureSource);
  const root = await realpath(
    await mkdtemp(join(tmpdir(), "omoc-commit-capture-")),
  );
  t.after(() => rm(root, { recursive: true, force: true }));
  const png = Buffer.alloc(24);
  Buffer.from("89504e470d0a1a0a", "hex").copy(png);
  png.writeUInt32BE(100, 16);
  png.writeUInt32BE(100, 20);
  const native = {
    png: png.toString("base64"),
    before: { visible: false, focused: false, focusable: false },
    osWindowVisible: false,
    osWindowFocused: false,
    osWindowFocusable: false,
    contentBounds: { width: 100, height: 100 },
    windowBounds: { width: 100, height: 100 },
    displayScaleFactor: 1,
    zoomFactor: 1,
  };
  const screenshots = [];
  const capture = new Function(
    "application",
    "assert",
    "join",
    "run",
    "writeFile",
    "screenshots",
    "createHash",
    `return (${captureSource});`,
  )(
    { evaluate: async () => native },
    assert,
    join,
    root,
    writeFile,
    screenshots,
    createHash,
  );
  const page = {
    evaluate: async () => ({ width: 100, height: 100, pixelRatio: 1 }),
  };
  await capture(page, "first.png", "first");
  await assert.rejects(capture(page, "first.png", "duplicate"), {
    code: "EEXIST",
  });
  assert.deepEqual(screenshots[0].afterCapture, {
    visible: false,
    focused: false,
    focusable: false,
  });
  assert.equal(
    screenshots[0].pngSha256,
    createHash("sha256").update(png).digest("hex"),
  );
  assert.match(screenshots[0].geometrySha256, /^[a-f0-9]{64}$/u);
  native.osWindowFocusable = true;
  await assert.rejects(capture(page, "focus.png", "focus"));
  native.osWindowFocusable = false;
  native.contentBounds.width = 1010;
  await assert.rejects(capture(page, "clipped.png", "clipped"));
  assert.equal(screenshots.length, 1);
});

for (const name of [
  "sha256",
  "unborn",
  "detached",
  "packed",
  "new-logs",
  "absent-logs",
  "disabled-existing-logs",
  "full-staged-deletion",
])
  test(
    "Commit actual controller closed state publication projection " + name,

          (i) => i.operation.type === "git.commit.apply",
        );
        assert.equal(intent.status, "accepted");
        assert.equal(intent.receipt.result.commitOid, p.commitOid);
        assert.deepEqual(
          intent.receipt.result.backups,
          commitPrivate.gitCommitExpectedBackups(
            commitContract.gitCommitTargetFromApply(intent.operation),
            intent.epoch,
            intent.id,
          ),
        );
        assert.equal(commitDraft(h).message, syntheticMessage);
        assert.equal(
          h.fixture.requests.filter((r) => r.type === "git.index.apply").length,
          0,
        );
      } finally {
        h.client.dispose();
      }
    },
  );
for (const name of ["denied", "unknown"])
  test(
    "Commit actual controller refusal leaves editable original draft " + name,
    async () => {
      const h = await commitController(name);
