        h.client.actions.editGitCommit({
          message: "SYNTHETIC edited after refusal",
        });
        assert.equal(commitDraft(h).message, "SYNTHETIC edited after refusal");
        if (name === "unknown")
          assert.equal(await h.client.actions.previewGitCommit(), false);
        assert.equal(await h.client.actions.applyGitCommit(), false);
        assert.equal(h.fixture.gitCommitEffects.length, 0);
      } finally {
        h.client.dispose();
      }
    },
  );
for (const boundary of [
  "workspace-ABA",
  "root-ABA",
  "trust-ABA",
  "epoch",
  "floor",
  "draft-ABA",
  "source",
  "sender-close",
])
  test(
    "Commit actual controller late preview cannot revive retired identity " +
      boundary,
    async () => {
      const h = await commitController("pending-preview");
      try {
        await h.client.actions.observeGitReview();
        h.client.actions.editGitCommit({
          ...syntheticAuthor,
          message: syntheticMessage,
        });
        const pending = h.client.actions.previewGitCommit();
        await extensionFlush();
        assert.equal(h.fixture.pendingGitCommitReadWorkspaces.size, 1);
        if (boundary === "workspace-ABA") {
          h.client.actions.select(
            "synthetic-index-other-w",
            "synthetic-index-other-s",
          );
          h.client.actions.select("fixture-w", "fixture-s");
        }
        if (boundary === "root-ABA") {
          h.fixture.snapshot.workspaces[0].path = "/synthetic/preview-root";
          h.seed();
          h.fixture.snapshot.workspaces[0].path = h.scope.workspacePath;
          h.seed();
        }
        if (boundary === "trust-ABA") {
          h.fixture.snapshot.workspaces[0].trusted = false;
          h.fixture.snapshot.workspaces[0].trustRevision++;
          h.seed();
          h.fixture.snapshot.workspaces[0].trusted = true;
          h.fixture.snapshot.workspaces[0].trustRevision++;
          h.seed();
        }
        if (boundary === "epoch") {
          h.fixture.snapshot.runtimeEpoch = "synthetic-preview-other-epoch";
          h.seed();
        }
        if (boundary === "floor") {
          h.fixture.snapshot.revision++;
          h.seed();
        }
        if (boundary === "draft-ABA") {
          h.client.actions.editGitCommit({
            message: "SYNTHETIC intervening edit",
          });
          h.client.actions.editGitCommit({ message: syntheticMessage });
        }
        if (boundary === "source")
          h.fixture.syntheticGitCommits.get("fixture-w").digest = "f".repeat(
            64,
          );
        if (boundary === "sender-close") h.transport.senderClosed();
        h.fixture.releaseGitCommitReads();
        assert.equal(await pending, false);
        await extensionFlush();
        assert.equal(commitCurrent(h), undefined);
        assert.equal(await h.client.actions.applyGitCommit(), false);
        assert.equal(h.fixture.gitCommitEffects.length, 0);
        assert.equal(h.fixture.pendingGitCommitReadWorkspaces.size, 0);
      } finally {
        h.fixture.releaseGitCommitReads();
        h.client.dispose();
      }
    },
  );
test("Commit fixture closed state inventory and preview eviction never mint new IO authority", async () => {
  const { GIT_COMMIT_UI_STATES } = await import("./workbench-host.mjs");
  for (const name of GIT_COMMIT_UI_STATES) {
    const h = setup();
    h.fixture.setGitCommitState(name);
    assert.equal(h.fixture.gitCommitReads.length, 0);
    assert.equal(h.fixture.gitCommitEffects.length, 0);
    assert.equal(
      h.fixture.syntheticGitCommits.get("fixture-w").syntheticOnly,
      true,
    );
  }
  const h = setup();
  assert.throws(() => h.fixture.setGitCommitState("guessed-api"));
  h.fixture.setGitCommitState("ready");
  const oldest = (await commitPreviewWire(h)).value;
  for (
    let n = 0;
    n < commitContract.GIT_COMMIT_LIMITS.previewsPerWorkspace;
    n++
  )
    await commitPreviewWire(h, { message: "SYNTHETIC " + n });
  assert.equal(
    h.fixture.gitCommitPreviews.size,
    commitContract.GIT_COMMIT_LIMITS.previewsPerWorkspace,
  );
  assert.equal(
    (await h.transport.handle(h.event, commitApplyWire(h, oldest))).ok,
    false,
  );
  assert.equal(h.fixture.gitCommitEffects.length, 0);
});
test("Commit fixture cross-lane exclusion and pre/post idle recheck leave no confirmed effects", async () => {
  for (const when of ["before-dispatch", "before-completion"]) {
    const h = setup();
    h.fixture.setGitCommitState("ready");
    const p = (await commitPreviewWire(h)).value;
    h.fixture.holdGitCommitApply = when === "before-completion";
    if (when === "before-dispatch")
      h.fixture.snapshot.sessions[0].status = "running";
    const pending = h.transport.handle(h.event, commitApplyWire(h, p));
    if (when === "before-completion") {
      assert.equal(h.fixture.pendingGitCommitWorkspaces.size, 1);
      assert.equal((await h.transport.handle(h.event, gitWire(h))).ok, false);
      h.fixture.snapshot.sessions[0].status = "running";
      h.fixture.releaseGitCommitApplies();
    }
    const r = await pending;
    assert.equal(
      r.receipt.effect,
      when === "before-dispatch" ? "none" : "unknown",
    );
    assert.equal(h.fixture.gitCommitEffects.length, 0);
  }
});

test("Commit fixture explicit post-success read preserves unstaged paths and reports new HEAD", async () => {
  const h = setup();
  h.fixture.setGitCommitState("ready");
  const p = (await commitPreviewWire(h)).value;
  const staged = Object.entries(
    h.fixture.syntheticGitIndexes.get("fixture-w").entries,
  ).map(([path, e]) => [path, e.mode, e.oid]);
  assert.equal(
    p.treeDigest,
    createHash("sha256").update(JSON.stringify(staged)).digest("hex"),
  );
  const originalFiles = globalThis.structuredClone(h.fixture.workspaceFiles);
  await h.transport.handle(h.event, commitApplyWire(h, p));
  const observed = (await gitStatusWire(h)).value;
  assert.equal(observed.repository.head, p.commitOid);
  assert.equal(observed.files.length, 2);
  assert.ok(
    observed.files.every(
      (f) => f.index === "none" && f.worktree === "modified",
    ),
  );
  assert.deepEqual(h.fixture.workspaceFiles, originalFiles);
  const noChanges = await h.transport.handle(
    h.event,
    gitWire(h, "git.commit.preview.read", {
      observationId: observed.observationId,
      author: syntheticAuthor,
      message: syntheticMessage,
    }),
  );
  assert.equal(noChanges.value.reason, "no_changes");
});

test("Commit runner capture executes native callback guards before and after capture without launching Electron", async () => {
  const source = await readFile(
    new URL("../e2e-git-commit.mjs", import.meta.url),
    "utf8",
  );
  const code = source.match(
    /async function capture\(page, name, state\) \{[\s\S]*?\n\}/u,
  )?.[0];
  assert.ok(code);
  const png = Buffer.alloc(24);
  Buffer.from("89504e470d0a1a0a", "hex").copy(png);
  png.writeUInt32BE(100, 16);
  png.writeUInt32BE(100, 20);
  let visible = false,
    focused = false,
    focusable = false,
    flipAfter = false,
    nativeCaptures = 0,
    writes = 0;
  const window = {
    isVisible: () => visible,
    isFocused: () => focused,
    isFocusable: () => focusable,
    getContentBounds: () => ({ width: 100, height: 100 }),
    getBounds: () => ({ width: 100, height: 100 }),
    webContents: {
      getZoomFactor: () => 1,
      capturePage: async (_clip, options) => {
        assert.equal(options.stayHidden, true);
        assert.equal(options.stayAwake, true);
        nativeCaptures++;
        if (flipAfter) focusable = true;
        return { toPNG: () => png };
      },
    },
  };
  const application = {
    evaluate: async (callback) =>
      callback({
        BrowserWindow: { getAllWindows: () => [window] },
        screen: { getDisplayMatching: () => ({ scaleFactor: 1 }) },
      }),
  };
  const capture = new Function(
    "application",
    "assert",
    "join",
    "run",
    "writeFile",
    "screenshots",
    "createHash",
    "return (" + code + ");",
  )(
    application,
    assert,
    join,
    "/synthetic/capture",
    async () => {
      writes++;
    },
    [],
    createHash,
  );
  const page = {
    evaluate: async () => ({ width: 100, height: 100, pixelRatio: 1 }),
  };
  for (const state of ["visible", "focused", "focusable"]) {
    visible = state === "visible";
    focused = state === "focused";
    focusable = state === "focusable";
    await assert.rejects(capture(page, state + ".png", state));
    assert.equal(nativeCaptures, 0);
    assert.equal(writes, 0);
  }
  visible = focused = focusable = false;
  flipAfter = true;
  await assert.rejects(capture(page, "after.png", "after"));
  assert.equal(nativeCaptures, 1);
  assert.equal(writes, 0);
  focusable = false;
  flipAfter = false;
  await capture(page, "valid.png", "valid");
  assert.equal(nativeCaptures, 2);
  assert.equal(writes, 1);
});

test("Commit actual controller lookup-confirmed receipt cannot release original actual call before finally", async () => {
  const h = await commitController();
  try {
    await controllerCommitPreview(h);
    h.fixture.holdGitCommitApply = true;
    const pending = h.client.actions.applyGitCommit();
    for (let n = 0; n < 100 && !h.fixture.heldGitCommitApplies.length; n++)
      await new Promise((resolve) => setTimeout(resolve, 2));
    h.fixture.confirmGitCommitApplies();
    h.client.start();
    h.client.actions.refresh();
    await extensionFlush();
    const key = JSON.stringify([
      h.fixture.intents.at(-1).runtimeEpoch,
      h.fixture.intents.at(-1).operationId,
    ]);
    assert.equal(h.client.store.getState().operations[key].status, "accepted");
    assert.ok(h.fixture.lookups.length > 0);
    assert.equal(h.fixture.pendingGitCommitWorkspaces.size, 1);
    assert.equal(Object.keys(h.client.store.getState().gitCommitIO).length, 1);
    assert.equal(await h.client.actions.observeGitReview(), false);
    h.fixture.releaseGitCommitApplies();
    assert.equal(await pending, true);
    h.seed();
    await extensionFlush();
    assert.equal(h.fixture.pendingGitCommitWorkspaces.size, 0);
    assert.equal(Object.keys(h.client.store.getState().gitCommitIO).length, 0);
    assert.equal(h.fixture.snapshot.retainedOperations.length, 0);
  } finally {
    h.fixture.releaseGitCommitApplies();
    h.client.dispose();
  }
});

test("Commit actual controller logical timeout preserves actual finally hold and original lookup key", async () => {
  const h = await commitController();
  try {
    await controllerCommitPreview(h);
    h.fixture.holdGitCommitApply = true;
    const pending = h.client.actions.applyGitCommit();
    for (let n = 0; n < 100 && !h.fixture.heldGitCommitApplies.length; n++)
      await new Promise((resolve) => setTimeout(resolve, 2));
    const timeout = [...h.timers].find(([, timer]) => timer.delay === 10000);
    assert.ok(timeout);
    h.timers.delete(timeout[0]);
    timeout[1].callback();
    assert.equal(await pending, false);
    await extensionFlush();
    assert.equal(h.fixture.pendingGitCommitWorkspaces.size, 1);
    assert.equal(Object.keys(h.client.store.getState().gitCommitIO).length, 1);
    assert.equal(await h.client.actions.previewGitCommit(), false);
    h.client.actions.editGitCommit({
      message: "SYNTHETIC editable after timeout",
    });
    h.fixture.confirmGitCommitApplies();
    h.client.start();
    h.client.actions.refresh();
    await extensionFlush();
    for (const [id, timer] of [...h.timers])
      if ([400, 5000].includes(timer.delay)) {
        h.timers.delete(id);
        timer.callback();
      }
    await extensionFlush();
    assert.equal(h.fixture.pendingGitCommitWorkspaces.size, 1);
    assert.equal(Object.keys(h.client.store.getState().gitCommitIO).length, 1);
    assert.equal(h.fixture.gitCommitEffects.length, 1);
    const original = h.fixture.intents.at(-1);
    assert.ok(
      h.fixture.lookupEnvelopes.every(
        (e) =>
          e.operation.runtimeEpoch === original.runtimeEpoch &&
          e.operation.operationId === original.operationId,
      ),
    );
    h.fixture.releaseGitCommitApplies();
    await extensionFlush();
    assert.equal(Object.keys(h.client.store.getState().gitCommitIO).length, 0);
    assert.equal(commitDraft(h).message, "SYNTHETIC editable after timeout");
    assert.equal(
      h.fixture.requests.filter((r) => r.type === "git.commit.apply").length,
      1,
    );
  } finally {
    h.fixture.releaseGitCommitApplies();
    h.client.dispose();
  }
});

// Branch/restore consumes the actual frozen closed producer API. Maps only.
const branchRestoreContract = await import(
  "../../src/renderer/shared/git-branch-restore/index.mjs"
);
const branchRestorePrivate = await import(
  "../../src/host/git-branch-restore-contract.mjs"
);
const branchRestoreStorage = await import(
  "../../src/host/workbench-git-branch-restore-storage.mjs"
);
async function branchRestorePreviewWire(
  h,
  action = "create",
  source = "index",
  paths = ["notes/SYNTHETIC-editor.txt"],
) {
  const observed = await gitStatusWire(h);
  assert.equal(observed.ok, true);
  return h.transport.handle(
    h.event,
    gitWire(
      h,
      action === "restore"
        ? "git.restore.preview.read"
        : "git.branch.preview.read",
      {
        observationId: observed.value.observationId,
        ...(action === "restore"
          ? { source, paths }
          : {
              action,
              branch:
                action === "create"
                  ? "refs/heads/SYNTHETIC-new"
                  : "refs/heads/review/diverged-local-tip",
              author: syntheticAuthor,
            }),
      },
    ),
  );
}
function branchRestoreApplyWire(
  p,
  operationId = "synthetic-branch-restore-apply",
) {
  return {
    version: 2,
    runtimeEpoch: p.runtimeEpoch,
    expectedRevision: p.revision,
    operationId,
    operation: {
      type: p.action === "restore" ? "git.restore.apply" : "git.branch.apply",
      workspaceId: p.workspaceId,
      requestId: operationId,
      previewId: p.previewId,
      ...Object.fromEntries(
        branchRestoreContract.GIT_BRANCH_RESTORE_TARGET_FIELDS.map((k) => [
          k,
          p[k],
        ]),
      ),
    },
  };
}
for (const action of ["create", "select", "restore"])
  test(
    "Branch/restore actual closed preview/apply and original backups: " +
      action,
    async () => {
      const h = setup();
      h.fixture.setGitBranchRestoreState(
        action === "restore" ? "restore-changes" : "ready",
      );
      assert.equal(h.fixture.gitBranchRestoreReads.length, 0);
      const original = globalThis.structuredClone(
        h.fixture.syntheticGitBranchRestores.get("fixture-w"),
      );
      const p = (await branchRestorePreviewWire(h, action)).value;
      branchRestoreContract.validateGitBranchRestorePreview(p);
      assert.equal(p.status, "available");
      const e = branchRestoreApplyWire(p),
        target = branchRestoreContract.gitBranchRestoreTargetFromApply(
          e.operation,
        );
      const r = await h.transport.handle(h.event, e);
      assert.equal(r.value.status, "applied");
      const digest = createHash("sha256")
        .update(
          JSON.stringify(
            Object.entries({
              ...e.operation,
              expectedRevision: p.revision,
            }).sort(([a], [b]) => (a < b ? -1 : a > b ? 1 : 0)),
          ),
        )
        .digest("hex");
      assert.equal(
        branchRestoreStorage.validGitBranchRestoreAppliedResult(
          r.receipt,
          target,
          digest,
        ),
        true,
      );
      assert.deepEqual(
        r.value.backups,
        branchRestorePrivate.gitBranchRestoreExpectedBackups(
          target,
          p.runtimeEpoch,
          e.operationId,
        ),
      );
      const next = h.fixture.syntheticGitBranchRestores.get("fixture-w");
      if (action === "create") {
        assert.equal(next.currentBranch, original.currentBranch);
        assert.deepEqual(next.worktree, original.worktree);
        assert.equal(next.indexDigest, original.indexDigest);
      }
      if (action === "select") {
        assert.equal(next.currentBranch, target.branch);
        assert.equal(next.headOid, target.nextHeadOid);
      }
      if (action === "restore") {
        assert.equal(next.headOid, original.headOid);
        assert.equal(next.indexDigest, original.indexDigest);
        assert.deepEqual(next.logs, original.logs);
      }
      assert.deepEqual(next.untracked, original.untracked);
      assert.deepEqual(next.ignored, original.ignored);
      assert.deepEqual(next.emptyDirectories, original.emptyDirectories);
      assert.equal(
        JSON.stringify(r.receipt).includes(syntheticAuthor.email),
        false,
      );
      assert.equal(h.fixture.gitBranchRestoreEffects.length, 1);
      await h.transport.handle(h.event, e);
      assert.equal(h.fixture.gitBranchRestoreEffects.length, 1);
    },
  );
for (const name of [
  "sha256",
  "detached",
  "packed",
  "new-logs",
  "absent-logs",
  "disabled-existing-logs",
])
  test(
    "Branch/restore format and nullable ordered metadata: " + name,
    async () => {
      const h = setup();
      h.fixture.setGitBranchRestoreState(name);
      const p = (await branchRestorePreviewWire(h, "select")).value;
      assert.equal(p.status, "available");
      assert.equal(p.headOid.length, name === "sha256" ? 64 : 40);
      assert.deepEqual(
        p.metadata.map((m) => m.role),
        name === "absent-logs"
          ? ["index", "head_ref"]
          : ["index", "head_log", "head_ref"],
      );
      const r = await h.transport.handle(h.event, branchRestoreApplyWire(p));
      assert.equal(r.value.status, "applied");
      if (name === "new-logs")
        assert.equal(
          r.value.backups.find((b) => b.role === "head_log").backupId,
          null,
        );
    },
  );
for (const source of ["index", "head"])
  test(
    "Branch/restore exact INDEX versus HEAD creation deletion mode and loss: " +
      source,
    async () => {
      const h = setup(),
        scope = h.fixture.setGitBranchRestoreState("restore-changes");
      const original = globalThis.structuredClone(
        h.fixture.syntheticGitBranchRestores.get("fixture-w"),
      );
      const p = (
        await branchRestorePreviewWire(h, "restore", source, scope.paths)
      ).value;
      assert.equal(p.status, "available");
      assert.equal(p.author, null);
      assert.deepEqual(p.metadata, []);
      assert.equal(p.paths[0].before, null);
      assert.equal(p.paths[0].after.mode, 0o600);
      const mode = p.paths.find((p) => p.path.endsWith(".sh"));
      assert.equal(mode.after.mode, source === "head" ? 0o750 : 0o640);
      const other = p.paths.find((p) => p.path.endsWith("other.txt"));
      assert.equal(other.after === null, source === "head");
      const r = await h.transport.handle(h.event, branchRestoreApplyWire(p));
      assert.equal(r.value.backups[0].backupId, null);
      assert.ok(r.value.backups.slice(1).every((b) => b.backupId !== null));
      const next = h.fixture.syntheticGitBranchRestores.get("fixture-w");
      assert.deepEqual(next.indexEntries, original.indexEntries);
      assert.deepEqual(next.logs, original.logs);
      assert.equal(Boolean(next.worktree[other.path]), source === "index");
    },
  );
for (const [name, action, reason] of [
  ["dirty", "select", "conflict"],
  ["branch_occupied", "select", "branch_occupied"],
  ["branch_conflict", "select", "branch_conflict"],
  ["branch_occupied", "create", "branch_conflict"],
  ["protected_source", "select", "protected_source"],
  ["unsupported_configuration", "create", "unsupported_configuration"],
  ["unborn", "create", "unsupported_branch_restore"],
  [
    "unsupported_branch_restore_state",
    "create",
    "unsupported_branch_restore_state",
  ],
])
  test(
    "Branch/restore closed refusal preserves synthetic source: " +
      name +
      action,
    async () => {
      const h = setup();
      h.fixture.setGitBranchRestoreState(name);
      const original = globalThis.structuredClone(
        h.fixture.syntheticGitBranchRestores,
      );
      const r = await branchRestorePreviewWire(h, action);
      assert.equal(r.ok, true);
      assert.equal(r.value.status, "unavailable");
      assert.equal(r.value.reason, reason);
      assert.deepEqual(h.fixture.syntheticGitBranchRestores, original);
      assert.equal(h.fixture.gitBranchRestoreEffects.length, 0);
    },
  );
for (const marker of branchRestorePrivate.GIT_BRANCH_RESTORE_STATE_MARKERS)
  test(
    "Branch/restore selected marker preview apply settlement: " + marker,
    async () => {
      const h = setup();
      h.fixture.setGitBranchRestoreState("ready");
      const original = h.fixture.syntheticGitBranchRestores.get("fixture-w");
      original.markers = [marker];
      assert.equal(
        (await branchRestorePreviewWire(h)).value.reason,
        "unsupported_branch_restore_state",
      );
      original.markers = [];
      let p = (await branchRestorePreviewWire(h)).value;
      original.markers = [marker];
      assert.equal(
        (await h.transport.handle(h.event, branchRestoreApplyWire(p))).code,
        "FILE_UNSUPPORTED",
      );
      original.markers = [];
      p = (await branchRestorePreviewWire(h)).value;
      h.fixture.holdGitBranchRestoreApply = true;
      const pending = h.transport.handle(
        h.event,
        branchRestoreApplyWire(p, "marker-late"),
      );
      original.markers = [marker];
      h.fixture.releaseGitBranchRestoreApplies();
      assert.equal((await pending).value.status, "unknown");
      assert.equal(h.fixture.gitBranchRestoreEffects.length, 0);
      assert.equal(h.fixture.syntheticGitBranchRestoreBackups.size, 0);
    },
  );
for (const outcome of ["denied", "unknown", "expired_preview", "lock_conflict"])
  test(
    "Branch/restore refused or unknown original-key no replay: " + outcome,
    async () => {
      const h = setup();
      h.fixture.setGitBranchRestoreState("ready");
      const p = (await branchRestorePreviewWire(h)).value,
        e = branchRestoreApplyWire(p);
      h.fixture.nextGitBranchRestoreApply = outcome;
      const r = await h.transport.handle(h.event, e);
      branchRestoreStorage.validateGitBranchRestoreReceipt(r.receipt);
      assert.equal(h.fixture.gitBranchRestoreEffects.length, 0);
      await h.transport.handle(h.event, e);
      assert.equal(h.fixture.gitBranchRestoreEffects.length, 0);
      if (outcome === "unknown") {
        const l = await h.transport.handle(h.event, {
          version: 2,
          runtimeEpoch: h.fixture.snapshot.runtimeEpoch,
          readId: "synthetic-branch-lookup",
          operation: {
            type: "operation.lookup",
            runtimeEpoch: p.runtimeEpoch,
            operationId: e.operationId,
          },
        });
        assert.equal(l.value.receipt.effect, "unknown");
      }
    },
  );
async function branchRestoreController(name = "ready") {
  const h = setup(),
    scope = h.fixture.setGitBranchRestoreState(name);
  return {
    ...(await extensionController("populated", { existing: h })),
    scope,
  };
}
const branchRestoreCurrent = (h) =>
  h.selectors.currentGitBranchRestorePreview(h.client.store.getState());
const branchRestoreDraft = (h) =>
  h.selectors.gitBranchRestoreDraft(h.client.store.getState());
async function controllerBranchRestorePreview(
  h,
  action = "create",
  source = "head",
) {
  assert.equal(await h.client.actions.observeGitReview(), true);
  h.client.actions.editGitBranchRestore({
    action,
    branch:
      action === "create"
        ? "refs/heads/SYNTHETIC-new"
        : "refs/heads/review/diverged-local-tip",
    ...syntheticAuthor,
    source,
    paths: h.scope.paths,
  });
  assert.equal(await h.client.actions.previewGitBranchRestore(), true);
  assert.equal(branchRestoreCurrent(h).status, "available");
}
for (const action of ["create", "select", "restore"])
  test(
    "Branch/restore actual controller explicit success invalidates requires new read: " +
      action,
    async () => {
      const h = await branchRestoreController(
        action === "restore" ? "restore-changes" : "ready",
      );
      try {
        h.client.start();
        h.refocus();
        h.client.actions.refresh();
        await extensionFlush();
        h.client.actions.editGitBranchRestore({
          action,
          branch: "refs/heads/SYNTHETIC-new",
          ...syntheticAuthor,
        });
        assert.equal(h.fixture.gitReads.length, 0);
        assert.equal(h.fixture.gitBranchRestoreReads.length, 0);
        await controllerBranchRestorePreview(h, action);
        assert.equal(await h.client.actions.applyGitBranchRestore(), true);
        assert.equal(branchRestoreCurrent(h), undefined);
        assert.equal(await h.client.actions.applyGitBranchRestore(), false);
        assert.equal(h.fixture.gitReads.length, 1);
        assert.equal(h.fixture.gitBranchRestoreEffects.length, 1);
        h.seed();
        assert.equal(await h.client.actions.observeGitReview(), true);
        assert.equal(h.fixture.gitReads.length, 2);
      } finally {
        h.client.dispose();
      }
    },
  );
for (const boundary of [
  "workspace-ABA",
  "root-ABA",
  "trust-ABA",
  "draft-ABA",
  "epoch",
  "floor",
  "source",
  "sender-close",
])
  test(
    "Branch/restore actual controller late preview isolated: " + boundary,
    async () => {
      const h = await branchRestoreController("pending-preview");
      try {
        await h.client.actions.observeGitReview();
        h.client.actions.editGitBranchRestore({
          action: "create",
          branch: "refs/heads/SYNTHETIC-new",
          ...syntheticAuthor,
        });
        const pending = h.client.actions.previewGitBranchRestore();
        await extensionFlush();
        assert.equal(h.fixture.pendingGitBranchRestoreReadWorkspaces.size, 1);
        if (boundary === "workspace-ABA") {
          h.client.actions.select(
            "synthetic-index-other-w",
            "synthetic-index-other-s",
          );
          h.client.actions.select("fixture-w", "fixture-s");
        }
        if (boundary === "root-ABA") {
          h.fixture.snapshot.workspaces[0].path = "/synthetic/other-root";
          h.seed();
          h.fixture.snapshot.workspaces[0].path = h.scope.workspacePath;
          h.seed();
        }
        if (boundary === "trust-ABA") {
          h.fixture.snapshot.workspaces[0].trusted = false;
          h.fixture.snapshot.workspaces[0].trustRevision++;
          h.seed();
          h.fixture.snapshot.workspaces[0].trusted = true;
          h.fixture.snapshot.workspaces[0].trustRevision++;
          h.seed();
        }
        if (boundary === "draft-ABA") {
          h.client.actions.editGitBranchRestore({
            branch: "refs/heads/intervening",
          });
          h.client.actions.editGitBranchRestore({
            branch: "refs/heads/SYNTHETIC-new",
          });
        }
        if (boundary === "epoch") {
          h.fixture.snapshot.runtimeEpoch = "synthetic-branch-other-epoch";
          h.seed();
        }
        if (boundary === "floor") {
          h.fixture.snapshot.revision++;
          h.seed();
        }
        if (boundary === "source")
          h.fixture.syntheticGitBranchRestores.get("fixture-w").digest =
            "f".repeat(64);
        if (boundary === "sender-close") h.transport.senderClosed();
        h.fixture.releaseGitBranchRestoreReads();
        assert.equal(await pending, false);
        await extensionFlush();
        assert.equal(branchRestoreCurrent(h), undefined);
        assert.equal(await h.client.actions.applyGitBranchRestore(), false);
        assert.equal(h.fixture.gitBranchRestoreEffects.length, 0);
        assert.equal(h.fixture.pendingGitBranchRestoreReadWorkspaces.size, 0);
      } finally {
        h.fixture.releaseGitBranchRestoreReads();
        h.client.dispose();
      }
    },
  );
for (const boundary of [
  "held",
  "global-held",
  "untrusted",
  "root",
  "epoch",
  "workspace",
])
  test(
    "Branch/restore actual controller inert editable old scope: " + boundary,
    async () => {
      const h = await branchRestoreController();
      try {
        await controllerBranchRestorePreview(h);
        const oldKey = h.selectors.currentGitBranchRestoreDraftKey(
          h.client.store.getState(),
        );
        if (boundary === "held")
          h.fixture.snapshot.recovery.resources.push({
            workspaceId: "fixture-w",
            state: "unavailable",
            reason: "unresolved_operation",
          });
        if (boundary === "global-held")
          h.fixture.snapshot.recovery.store.state = "unavailable";
        if (boundary === "untrusted") {
          h.fixture.snapshot.workspaces[0].trusted = false;
          h.fixture.snapshot.workspaces[0].trustRevision++;
        }
        if (boundary === "root")
          h.fixture.snapshot.workspaces[0].path =
            "/synthetic/replaced-branch-root";
        if (boundary === "epoch")
          h.fixture.snapshot.runtimeEpoch = "synthetic-replaced-branch-epoch";
        if (boundary === "workspace")
          h.client.actions.select(
            "synthetic-index-other-w",
            "synthetic-index-other-s",
          );
        else {
          h.fixture.snapshot.revision++;
          h.seed();
        }
        if (["root", "epoch", "workspace"].includes(boundary))
          h.client.actions.selectGitBranchRestoreDraft(oldKey);
        h.client.actions.editGitBranchRestore({
          branch: "refs/heads/SYNTHETIC-editable",
        });
        assert.equal(
          branchRestoreDraft(h).branch,
          "refs/heads/SYNTHETIC-editable",
        );
        assert.equal(branchRestoreCurrent(h), undefined);
        assert.equal(await h.client.actions.applyGitBranchRestore(), false);
        assert.equal(h.fixture.gitBranchRestoreEffects.length, 0);
      } finally {
        h.client.dispose();
      }
    },
  );
for (const name of ["unknown", "retained"])
  test(
    "Branch/restore actual controller current-envelope original-key lookup only: " +
      name,
    async () => {
      const h = await branchRestoreController(name);
      try {
        if (name === "unknown") {
          await controllerBranchRestorePreview(h);
          assert.equal(await h.client.actions.applyGitBranchRestore(), false);
        }
        h.client.start();
        h.client.actions.refresh();
        h.refocus();
        await extensionFlush();
        for (const [id, timer] of [...h.timers])
          if ([400, 5000].includes(timer.delay)) {
            h.timers.delete(id);
            timer.callback();
          }
        await extensionFlush();
        assert.ok(h.fixture.lookups.length > 0);
        const key =
          name === "retained"
            ? h.fixture.gitBranchRestoreRetainedKey
            : h.fixture.intents.at(-1);
        assert.ok(
          h.fixture.lookupEnvelopes.every(
            (e) =>
              e.runtimeEpoch === h.fixture.snapshot.runtimeEpoch &&
              e.operation.runtimeEpoch === key.runtimeEpoch &&
              e.operation.operationId === key.operationId,
          ),
        );
        h.client.actions.editGitBranchRestore({
          branch: "refs/heads/SYNTHETIC-held-editable",
        });
        assert.equal(
          branchRestoreDraft(h).branch,
          "refs/heads/SYNTHETIC-held-editable",
        );
        assert.equal(
          h.fixture.requests.filter((r) =>
            ["git.branch.apply", "git.restore.apply"].includes(r.type),
          ).length,
          name === "unknown" ? 1 : 0,
        );
        assert.equal(h.fixture.gitBranchRestoreEffects.length, 0);
      } finally {
        h.client.dispose();
      }
    },
  );
for (const boundary of ["newer-draft", "workspace", "root", "epoch"])
  test(
    "Branch/restore actual controller confirmed lookup holds actual invocation late ACK: " +
      boundary,
    async () => {
      const h = await branchRestoreController();
      try {
        await controllerBranchRestorePreview(h);
        h.fixture.holdGitBranchRestoreApply = true;
        const pending = h.client.actions.applyGitBranchRestore();
        for (
          let n = 0;
          n < 100 && !h.fixture.heldGitBranchRestoreApplies.length;
          n++
        )
          await new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, 2));
        assert.equal(h.fixture.heldGitBranchRestoreApplies.length, 1);
        h.fixture.confirmGitBranchRestoreApplies();
        h.seed();
        await extensionFlush();
        assert.equal(h.fixture.pendingGitBranchRestoreWorkspaces.size, 1);
        assert.equal(
          Object.keys(h.client.store.getState().gitBranchRestoreIO).length,
          1,
        );
        assert.equal(await h.client.actions.observeGitReview(), false);
        if (boundary === "workspace")
          h.client.actions.select(
            "synthetic-index-other-w",
            "synthetic-index-other-s",
          );
        if (boundary === "root")
          h.fixture.snapshot.workspaces[0].path = "/synthetic/late-branch-root";
        if (boundary === "epoch")
          h.fixture.snapshot.runtimeEpoch = "synthetic-late-branch-epoch";
        if (["root", "epoch"].includes(boundary)) {
          h.fixture.snapshot.revision++;
          h.seed();
        }
        h.client.actions.editGitBranchRestore({
          branch: "refs/heads/SYNTHETIC-newer-draft",
        });
        h.fixture.releaseGitBranchRestoreApplies();
        await pending;
        await extensionFlush();
        assert.equal(h.fixture.pendingGitBranchRestoreWorkspaces.size, 0);
        assert.equal(
          Object.keys(h.client.store.getState().gitBranchRestoreIO).length,
          0,
        );
        assert.equal(
          branchRestoreDraft(h).branch,
          "refs/heads/SYNTHETIC-newer-draft",
        );
        assert.equal(h.fixture.gitBranchRestoreEffects.length, 1);
        assert.equal(
          h.fixture.requests.filter((r) => r.type === "git.branch.apply")
            .length,
          1,
        );
      } finally {
        h.fixture.releaseGitBranchRestoreApplies();
        h.client.dispose();
      }
    },
  );

test("Branch/restore runner capture retains exclusive PNGs and refuses focus or incomplete surfaces", async (t) => {
  const source = await readFile(
    new URL("../e2e-git-branch-restore.mjs", import.meta.url),
    "utf8",
  );
  const captureSource = source.match(
    /async function capture\(page, name, state\) \{[\s\S]*?\n\}/u,
  )?.[0];
  assert.ok(captureSource);
  const root = await realpath(
    await mkdtemp(join(tmpdir(), "omoc-branch-restore-capture-")),
  );
  t.after(() => rm(root, { recursive: true, force: true }));
  const png = Buffer.alloc(24);
  Buffer.from("89504e470d0a1a0a", "hex").copy(png);
  png.writeUInt32BE(100, 16);
  png.writeUInt32BE(100, 20);
  const native = {
    png: png.toString("base64"),
    before: { visible: false, focused: false, focusable: false },
    osWindowVisible: false,
    osWindowFocused: false,
    osWindowFocusable: false,
    contentBounds: { width: 100, height: 100 },
    windowBounds: { width: 100, height: 100 },
    displayScaleFactor: 1,
    zoomFactor: 1,
  };
  const screenshots = [];
  const capture = new Function(
    "application",
    "assert",
    "join",
    "run",
    "writeFile",
    "screenshots",
    "createHash",
    `return (${captureSource});`,
  )(
    { evaluate: async () => native },
    assert,
    join,
    root,
    writeFile,
    screenshots,
    createHash,
  );
  const page = {
    evaluate: async () => ({ width: 100, height: 100, pixelRatio: 1 }),
  };
  await capture(page, "first.png", "first");
  await assert.rejects(capture(page, "first.png", "duplicate"), {
    code: "EEXIST",
  });
  assert.deepEqual(screenshots[0].afterCapture, {
    visible: false,
    focused: false,
    focusable: false,
  });
  assert.equal(
    screenshots[0].pngSha256,
    createHash("sha256").update(png).digest("hex"),
  );
  assert.match(screenshots[0].geometrySha256, /^[a-f0-9]{64}$/u);
  native.osWindowFocusable = true;
  await assert.rejects(capture(page, "focus.png", "focus"));
  native.osWindowFocusable = false;
  native.contentBounds.width = 1010;
  await assert.rejects(capture(page, "clipped.png", "clipped"));
  assert.equal(screenshots.length, 1);
});

test("Branch/restore runner capture executes native callback guards before and after capture without launching Electron", async () => {
  const source = await readFile(
    new URL("../e2e-git-branch-restore.mjs", import.meta.url),
    "utf8",
  );
  const code = source.match(
    /async function capture\(page, name, state\) \{[\s\S]*?\n\}/u,
  )?.[0];
  assert.ok(code);
  const png = Buffer.alloc(24);
  Buffer.from("89504e470d0a1a0a", "hex").copy(png);
  png.writeUInt32BE(100, 16);
  png.writeUInt32BE(100, 20);
  let visible = false,
    focused = false,
    focusable = false,
    flipAfter = false,
    nativeCaptures = 0,
    writes = 0;
  const window = {
    isVisible: () => visible,
    isFocused: () => focused,
    isFocusable: () => focusable,
    getContentBounds: () => ({ width: 100, height: 100 }),
    getBounds: () => ({ width: 100, height: 100 }),
    webContents: {
      getZoomFactor: () => 1,
      capturePage: async (_clip, options) => {
        assert.equal(options.stayHidden, true);
        assert.equal(options.stayAwake, true);
        nativeCaptures++;
        if (flipAfter) focusable = true;
        return { toPNG: () => png };
      },
    },
  };
  const application = {
    evaluate: async (callback) =>
      callback({
        BrowserWindow: { getAllWindows: () => [window] },
        screen: { getDisplayMatching: () => ({ scaleFactor: 1 }) },
      }),
  };
  const capture = new Function(
    "application",
    "assert",
    "join",
    "run",
    "writeFile",
    "screenshots",
    "createHash",
    "return (" + code + ");",
  )(
    application,
    assert,
    join,
    "/synthetic/capture",
    async () => {
      writes++;
    },
    [],
    createHash,
  );
  const page = {
    evaluate: async () => ({ width: 100, height: 100, pixelRatio: 1 }),
  };
  for (const state of ["visible", "focused", "focusable"]) {
    visible = state === "visible";
    focused = state === "focused";
    focusable = state === "focusable";
    await assert.rejects(capture(page, state + ".png", state));
    assert.equal(nativeCaptures, 0);
    assert.equal(writes, 0);
  }
  visible = focused = focusable = false;
  flipAfter = true;
  await assert.rejects(capture(page, "after.png", "after"));
  assert.equal(nativeCaptures, 1);
  assert.equal(writes, 0);
  focusable = false;
  flipAfter = false;
  await capture(page, "valid.png", "valid");
  assert.equal(nativeCaptures, 2);
  assert.equal(writes, 1);
});

test("Branch/restore actual controller logical timeout preserves actual finally hold and original lookup key", async () => {
  const h = await branchRestoreController();
  try {
    await controllerBranchRestorePreview(h);
    h.fixture.holdGitBranchRestoreApply = true;
    const pending = h.client.actions.applyGitBranchRestore();
    for (
      let n = 0;
      n < 100 && !h.fixture.heldGitBranchRestoreApplies.length;
      n++
    )
      await new Promise((resolve) => setTimeout(resolve, 2));
    const timeout = [...h.timers].find(([, timer]) => timer.delay === 10000);
    assert.ok(timeout);
    h.timers.delete(timeout[0]);
    timeout[1].callback();
    assert.equal(await pending, false);
    await extensionFlush();
    assert.equal(h.fixture.pendingGitBranchRestoreWorkspaces.size, 1);
    assert.equal(
      Object.keys(h.client.store.getState().gitBranchRestoreIO).length,
      1,
    );
    assert.equal(await h.client.actions.previewGitBranchRestore(), false);
    h.client.actions.editGitBranchRestore({
      branch: "refs/heads/SYNTHETIC-after-timeout",
    });
    h.fixture.confirmGitBranchRestoreApplies();
    h.client.start();
    h.client.actions.refresh();
    await extensionFlush();
    for (const [id, timer] of [...h.timers])
      if ([400, 5000].includes(timer.delay)) {
        h.timers.delete(id);
        timer.callback();
      }
    await extensionFlush();
    assert.equal(h.fixture.pendingGitBranchRestoreWorkspaces.size, 1);
    assert.equal(
      Object.keys(h.client.store.getState().gitBranchRestoreIO).length,
      1,
    );
    assert.equal(h.fixture.gitBranchRestoreEffects.length, 1);
    const original = h.fixture.intents.at(-1);
    assert.ok(
      h.fixture.lookupEnvelopes.every(
        (e) =>
          e.operation.runtimeEpoch === original.runtimeEpoch &&
          e.operation.operationId === original.operationId,
      ),
    );
    h.fixture.releaseGitBranchRestoreApplies();
    await extensionFlush();
    assert.equal(
      Object.keys(h.client.store.getState().gitBranchRestoreIO).length,
      0,
    );
    assert.equal(
      branchRestoreDraft(h).branch,
      "refs/heads/SYNTHETIC-after-timeout",
    );
    assert.equal(
      h.fixture.requests.filter((r) => r.type === "git.branch.apply").length,
      1,
    );
  } finally {
    h.fixture.releaseGitBranchRestoreApplies();
    h.client.dispose();
  }
});
test("Branch/restore receipt cannot self-authorize digest, target, original key, or backup loss", async () => {
  const h = setup();
  h.fixture.setGitBranchRestoreState("restore-changes");
  const p = (
    await branchRestorePreviewWire(h, "restore", "head", [
      "notes/SYNTHETIC-editor.txt",
      "notes/SYNTHETIC-mode.sh",
      "notes/SYNTHETIC-other.txt",
    ])
  ).value;
  const e = branchRestoreApplyWire(p),
    t = branchRestoreContract.gitBranchRestoreTargetFromApply(e.operation),
    r = (await h.transport.handle(h.event, e)).receipt;
  for (const change of [
    (r) => (r.result.digest = "f".repeat(64)),
    (r) => (r.result.previewDigest = "e".repeat(64)),
    (r) => (r.runtimeEpoch = "foreign-epoch"),
    (r) => (r.operationId = "foreign-op"),
    (r) => (r.result.paths[1].after.mode = 0o640),
    (r) =>
      (r.result.backups[0].backupId =
        branchRestorePrivate.gitBranchRestoreBackupId(
          "foreign-epoch",
          "foreign-op",
          "worktree",
          p.paths[0].path,
        )),
    (r) => r.result.backups.pop(),
  ]) {
    const forged = globalThis.structuredClone(r);
    change(forged);
    assert.equal(
      branchRestoreStorage.validGitBranchRestoreAppliedResult(
        forged,
        t,
        r.result.digest,
      ),
      false,
    );
  }
  for (const b of r.result.backups)
    if (b.backupId) {
      const saved = h.fixture.syntheticGitBranchRestoreBackups.get(b.backupId);
      assert.deepEqual(
        saved.original,
        t.paths.find((p) => p.path === b.path).before,
      );
    }
  assert.equal(JSON.stringify(r).includes("/synthetic/"), false);
});
test("Branch/restore validates explicit protected and canonical path requests without effects", async () => {
  const h = setup();
  h.fixture.setGitBranchRestoreState("restore-changes");
  for (const paths of [
    ["AGENTS.md"],
    ["../outside"],
    ["notes/SYNTHETIC-other.txt", "notes/SYNTHETIC-editor.txt"],
    ["notes/SYNTHETIC-editor.txt", "notes/SYNTHETIC-editor.txt"],
  ]) {
    const observed = await gitStatusWire(h);
    const r = await h.transport.handle(
      h.event,
      gitWire(h, "git.restore.preview.read", {
        observationId: observed.value.observationId,
        source: "head",
        paths,
      }),
    );
    assert.equal(r.code, "INVALID_REQUEST");
  }
  const p = (
    await branchRestorePreviewWire(h, "restore", "index", [
      "notes/SYNTHETIC-no-index.txt",
    ])
  ).value;
  assert.equal(p.reason, "missing_file");
  assert.equal(h.fixture.gitBranchRestoreEffects.length, 0);
});
test("Branch/restore cancelled preview holds actual lane until release and sender-bound preview refuses other transport", async () => {
  const h = await branchRestoreController("pending-preview");
  try {
    await h.client.actions.observeGitReview();
    h.client.actions.editGitBranchRestore({
      action: "create",
      branch: "refs/heads/SYNTHETIC-new",
      ...syntheticAuthor,
    });
    const pending = h.client.actions.previewGitBranchRestore();
    await extensionFlush();
    h.client.actions.cancelGitBranchRestorePreview();
    assert.equal(await pending, false);
    assert.equal(h.fixture.pendingGitBranchRestoreReadWorkspaces.size, 1);
    assert.equal(await h.client.actions.previewGitBranchRestore(), false);
    h.fixture.releaseGitBranchRestoreReads();
    await extensionFlush();
    assert.equal(h.fixture.pendingGitBranchRestoreReadWorkspaces.size, 0);
    assert.equal(await h.client.actions.previewGitBranchRestore(), true);
    const p = branchRestoreCurrent(h),
      other = createFixtureTransport(h.fixture, {
        webContents: h.event.sender,
        allowedUrl: (url) => url === "file:///fixture/index.html",
      });
    assert.equal(
      (await other.handle(h.event, branchRestoreApplyWire(p))).code,
      "FILE_CHANGED",
    );
    assert.equal(h.fixture.gitBranchRestoreEffects.length, 0);
  } finally {
    h.fixture.releaseGitBranchRestoreReads();
    h.client.dispose();
  }
});
test("Branch/restore preview cache is bounded and imports/state selection are inert", async () => {
  const h = setup();
  h.fixture.setGitBranchRestoreState("ready");
  assert.throws(() =>
    h.fixture.setGitBranchRestoreState("guessed-production-api"),
  );
  assert.equal(h.fixture.gitBranchRestoreReads.length, 0);
  assert.equal(h.fixture.gitBranchRestoreEffects.length, 0);
  for (
    let n = 0;
    n <
    branchRestoreContract.GIT_BRANCH_RESTORE_LIMITS.previewsPerWorkspace + 3;
    n++
  )
    await branchRestorePreviewWire(h);
  assert.equal(
    h.fixture.gitBranchRestorePreviews.size,
    branchRestoreContract.GIT_BRANCH_RESTORE_LIMITS.previewsPerWorkspace,
  );
  assert.equal(h.fixture.gitBranchRestoreEffects.length, 0);
});

test("Branch/restore actual controller lookup-confirmed receipt cannot release original actual call before finally", async () => {
  const h = await branchRestoreController();
  try {
    await controllerBranchRestorePreview(h);
    h.fixture.holdGitBranchRestoreApply = true;
    const pending = h.client.actions.applyGitBranchRestore();
    for (
      let n = 0;
      n < 100 && !h.fixture.heldGitBranchRestoreApplies.length;
      n++
    )
      await new Promise((resolve) => setTimeout(resolve, 2));
    h.fixture.confirmGitBranchRestoreApplies();
    h.client.start();
    h.client.actions.refresh();
    await extensionFlush();
    const key = JSON.stringify([
      h.fixture.intents.at(-1).runtimeEpoch,
      h.fixture.intents.at(-1).operationId,
    ]);
    assert.equal(h.client.store.getState().operations[key].status, "accepted");
    assert.ok(h.fixture.lookups.length > 0);
    assert.equal(h.fixture.pendingGitBranchRestoreWorkspaces.size, 1);
    assert.equal(
      Object.keys(h.client.store.getState().gitBranchRestoreIO).length,
      1,
    );
    assert.equal(await h.client.actions.observeGitReview(), false);
    h.fixture.releaseGitBranchRestoreApplies();
    assert.equal(await pending, true);
    h.seed();
    await extensionFlush();
    assert.equal(h.fixture.pendingGitBranchRestoreWorkspaces.size, 0);
    assert.equal(
      Object.keys(h.client.store.getState().gitBranchRestoreIO).length,
      0,
    );
    assert.equal(h.fixture.snapshot.retainedOperations.length, 0);
  } finally {
    h.fixture.releaseGitBranchRestoreApplies();
    h.client.dispose();
  }
});
